## Review boundary and packet completeness

Define the new review subject, required outcome, trust/failure boundaries and
excluded acceptance claims. Separate source, actual-result and document review

including unexpected defects and omitted dependencies.

Every packet has a coverage ledger with these columns:

| Requirement / threat | Changed source or contract | Necessary dependency context | Test / primary evidence and limits | Reviewer disposition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Explicit task requirement | Exact path + SHA256 + rendered region/full body | Relevant producer/consumer/authority boundary | Actual observed evidence; pending where absent | Reviewer assesses coverage and findings |

Before dispatch, mechanically check required bodies/edges, hashes and declared
evidence; fail closed on missing/stale inputs. These checks establish identity
and declared completeness, not correctness or sufficiency of the declaration.

unrendered records honestly; an unrendered body is not newly reviewed.

provenance. Identify that verdict and preserve unresolved findings and every
material limitation. Hash equality proves identity, not correctness or a new
acceptance. In particular, the owner-accepted combined source51 basis is not
Claude SOURCE Review OK; read51 RESULT Review OK is not whole-Foundation proof.

explicit prior-disposition/limitations ledger and exact references, provided

beyond the executor's list. A repair round does not silently accept omitted code.

Do not instruct the reviewer to trust reassuring labels or restrict findings to

deployment, live proof and owner acceptance.

Ask for a concise visible response: exact scope/verdict, coverage gaps, actionable
findings with evidence, and material limits. Preserve the complete actual response
and run/dispatch/model/input identity. Do not truncate the response to save tokens
or impose a ceiling that blocks necessary analysis. CLI token/cost statistics do
not measure subscription percentage or prove a paid API charge.

===== FULL BODY docs/WORK_INTERRUPTION_RECOVERY.md; ORIGINAL BUFFER SHA256 9DDBA7C2724B779FB4E6EDAC1963DF5C4D826028EE3851C0250ECCCE11714136; BYTES 4364 =====
# Continuous recovery during long work

Date: 2026-10-09. Authority: direct owner clarification in the active GSS chat.
Applies to the executor's ongoing work in this repository, including long GSS
segments. Codex remains the assigned GSS writer; actual Claude reviews. This is
a working procedure, not additional implementation, native or publication authority.

## Owner intent

Prevent losing substantial progress when electricity fails or the PC stops
unexpectedly at ANY point in a long work segment. A checkpoint only at the end
of a session does not meet this requirement. The clarification is effective
for subsequent work; earlier one-time checkpoints remain historical evidence.

## Save continuously

- Save task-owned code and documents to files as they are edited. Keep partial
  work on disk, clearly labelled incomplete/unverified; do not retain an entire
  implementation only in chat, model reasoning or process memory.
- Record a small recovery checkpoint after each meaningful completed substep,
  before a long test/review/external operation, after its result, and before
  handing off or finishing. During longer active work, refresh it at least
  every 10 minutes at the next controllable tool boundary, even if incomplete.
  Do not wait for the whole block to finish. This cadence is executor procedure,
  not an installed background timer or guarantee that a blocked process can
  checkpoint itself. Long commands should yield control while running.
- For a long process, save a dispatch/start record first. Retain bounded safe
  progress/output on disk as available; flush operation journals and reservations
  at their protocol-required boundaries. Save a terminal result only once it
  is observed. Where progress cannot be captured during execution, record that
  limit and treat an interrupted operation as unresolved.
- Store completed steps, work in progress, saved artifact paths, latest check
  and review evidence, pending decisions, launched operations/consumed grants,
  and the exact next bounded action. Reference existing evidence rather than
  duplicating large manifests or private data in every checkpoint.
- Keep dated snapshots; advance the task-owned current pointer only after the
  new checkpoint has been written, fsynced and read back. Use an atomic file
  replacement with an unchanged-old-pointer check. A progress update does not
  replace the last accepted result or grant acceptance/authority.

For current GSS work use `handoffs/GSS_foundation_RESUME_CURRENT.md`. The additive
helper `work/save-gss-progress-checkpoint_20261009.mjs` accepts a small saved
public JSON state file under `work/`, writes a dated checkpoint/receipt, and
advances ONLY that pointer. It executes no native operation and is not a daemon.
The executor must invoke it at the boundaries above. Other task writers keep
their own separate checkpoints; never advance another writer's pointer.
This helper version anchors the accepted read51 result. After a newer accepted
stage, prepare an additive successor with that stage's exact anchor; do not keep
labelling read51 as the latest accepted stage or edit a frozen helper in place.

## Resume after interruption

Read the current task pointer, its dated snapshot, the last accepted result,
current project rules and preserved operation evidence before doing work. Verify
the relevant files and current environment. A dispatch without a verified
terminal result is an interrupted/uncertain operation, not success or proof it
never ran. Inspect its journal/reservation and reconcile in the authorized scope
before proceeding. Never automatically replay a write, a one-shot command or a
consumed grant. Historical container health is not current health after reboot.

Preserve frozen material and unrelated work. Checkpointing does not authorize
editing a pinned instruction/source, restoring containers, status changes,
production writes, new grants, Git publication or a change of reviewer format.
No secrets, private accounts, customer data or credential file contents belong
in a progress checkpoint. Local fsync/readback reduces exposure to interruption;
it does not provide a remote backup, physical-disk-failure protection, or an
absolute promise of zero lost in-flight reasoning/work. The recovery boundary
is the last durable snapshot plus the saved artifacts and operation records.

===== END BODY =====

===== FULL BODY work/gss-CR03-public-artifact-guard_20261011.mjs; ORIGINAL BUFFER SHA256 05EA420166D79D9899DE941FF1768B705643E27D49EA108F02F2E46C614AFAEC; BYTES 1421 =====
// Public local output hygiene only. Not a cross-process lock or recovery proof.
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
const ROOT='C:\\Users\\14bez\\Downloads\\Booster Shop\\booster-shop-ops';
export function assertCaseUniquePaths(proposed,existing=[]){
  const key=p=>{assert.equal(typeof p,'string');assert(/^(work|plans|diagnostics)\/[A-Za-z0-9_.-]+\.(mjs|md|json|txt)$/i.test(p),'Explicit top-level public output required');return p.toLowerCase();};
  const seen=new Set(existing.map(key));
  for(const p of proposed){const k=key(p);assert(!seen.has(k),'Case-insensitive public output collision: '+p);seen.add(k);}
  return true;
}
export function writePublicArtifact(relative,bytes){
  assert.equal(process.cwd(),ROOT);
  assertCaseUniquePaths([relative]);
  const parent=path.dirname(relative),folder=path.join(ROOT,parent);
  assert.equal(fs.realpathSync.native(folder),folder);
  const names=fs.readdirSync(folder).filter(n=>/^[A-Za-z0-9_.-]+\.(mjs|md|json|txt)$/i.test(n)).map(n=>parent+'/'+n);
  assertCaseUniquePaths([relative],names);
  const target=path.join(ROOT,relative),b=Buffer.from(bytes),fd=fs.openSync(target,'wx');
  try{let offset=0;while(offset<b.length){const written=fs.writeSync(fd,b,offset,b.length-offset);assert(written>0);offset+=written;}fs.fsyncSync(fd);}finally{fs.closeSync(fd);}
  assert(fs.readFileSync(target).equals(b));
}

// Exact task-owned fictional email permitted in packet and result. No account grant.
export const permittedPublicLiterals=Object.freeze(['fictional@example.invalid']);
const patterns=[['ANTHROPIC_KEY',/sk-ant-[A-Za-z0-9_-]{20,}/g],['API_KEY',/sk-(?:proj-)?[A-Za-z0-9_-]{40,}/g],['JWT',/eyJ[A-Za-z0-9_-]{12,}\.[A-Za-z0-9_-]{12,}\.[A-Za-z0-9_-]{12,}/g],['PRIVATE_KEY_HEADER',/-----BEGIN (?:RSA |EC |OPENSSH )?PRIVATE KEY-----/g],['EMAIL',/[A-Za-z0-9._%+-]+@[A-Za-z0-9.-]+\.[A-Za-z]{2,}/g],['AUTH_URL_CODE',/[?&](?:access_token|refresh_token|code)=[A-Za-z0-9_-]{12,}/gi]];
export function sensitiveSpans(bytes,{escapeAware=true}={}){
 assert(Buffer.isBuffer(bytes));const text=bytes.toString('latin1'),found=[];
 for(const [patternId,re]of patterns){re.lastIndex=0;for(const m of text.matchAll(re)){
  let start=m.index;
  if(escapeAware&&start>0&&text[start-1]==='\\'){
   let n=0;for(let i=start-1;i>=0&&text[i]==='\\';i--)n++;
   if(n%2===1){if(/^u[0-9A-Fa-f]{4}/.test(text.slice(start)))start+=5;else if('ntrbf'.includes(text[start]))start++;}
  }
  const end=m.index+m[0].length;if(start>=end)continue;
  if(patternId==='EMAIL'&&permittedPublicLiterals.includes(text.slice(start,end)))continue;
  found.push({startByte:start,endByte:end,patternIds:[patternId]});
 }}
 found.sort((a,b)=>a.startByte-b.startByte||a.endByte-b.endByte);const merged=[];
 for(const s of found){const last=merged.at(-1);if(last&&s.startByte<last.endByte){last.endByte=Math.max(last.endByte,s.endByte);last.patternIds=[...new Set([...last.patternIds,...s.patternIds])];}else merged.push({...s});}
 // No original span hash: low-entropy value confirmation is deliberately withheld.
 return merged;
}
export function redactResponseBytes(bytes,{markerFactory=i=>'<REDACTED:'+i+'>',...options}={}){
 const spans=sensitiveSpans(bytes,options),parts=[];let at=0;
 const publicSpans=spans.map((s,i)=>({...s,marker:markerFactory(i)}));
 for(const s of publicSpans){parts.push(bytes.subarray(at,s.startByte),Buffer.from(s.marker));at=s.endByte;}parts.push(bytes.subarray(at));
 const publicBytes=Buffer.concat(parts);return {publicBytes,spans:publicSpans,originalSha256:spans.length?null:hash(bytes),publicSha256:hash(publicBytes),originalRetainedBytes:bytes.length,mode:'BYTE_FALLBACK',coordinate:'ORIGINAL_RETAINED_BYTE_OFFSETS'};
}
function redactJsonEnvelope(bytes,nextMarker){
 let value;try{value=JSON.parse(new TextDecoder('utf8',{fatal:true,ignoreBOM:true}).decode(bytes));}catch{return null;}
 try{
  const spans=[];
  function string(s,entryPath){
   const r=redactResponseBytes(Buffer.from(s),{escapeAware:false,markerFactory:()=>nextMarker('VALUE')});
   for(const span of r.spans)spans.push({...span,entryPath,kind:'VALUE',coordinate:'DECODED_STRING_UTF8_BYTE_OFFSETS'});
   // Preserve unrelated strings, including lone surrogates, in the parsed value.
   return r.spans.length?r.publicBytes.toString('utf8'):s;
  }
  function visit(v,at){
   if(typeof v==='string')return string(v,at);
   if(Array.isArray(v))return v.map((x,i)=>visit(x,at+'[array:'+i+']'));
   if(v&&typeof v==='object'){
    const keys=Object.keys(v),reserved=new Set(keys),out=Object.create(null);
    for(const [i,k]of keys.entries()){
     const entryPath=at+'[object-entry:'+i+']',keySpans=sensitiveSpans(Buffer.from(k),{escapeAware:false});let publicKey=k;
     if(keySpans.length){do{publicKey=nextMarker('KEY');}while(reserved.has(publicKey)||Object.hasOwn(out,publicKey));
      for(const span of keySpans)spans.push({...span,marker:publicKey,entryPath,kind:'KEY',coordinate:'DECODED_STRING_UTF8_BYTE_OFFSETS'});
     }
     out[publicKey]=visit(v[k],entryPath+'.value');
    }return out;
   }return v;
  }
  const publicValue=visit(value,'$'),publicBytes=spans.length?Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(publicValue)):Buffer.from(bytes);
  return {publicBytes,spans,originalSha256:spans.length?null:hash(bytes),publicSha256:hash(publicBytes),originalRetainedBytes:bytes.length,mode:'JSON_ENVELOPE',coordinate:'DECODED_STRING_UTF8_BYTE_OFFSETS'};
 }catch(e){
  const r=redactResponseBytes(bytes,{escapeAware:true,markerFactory:()=>nextMarker('VALUE')});
  return {...r,mode:'BYTE_FALLBACK_AFTER_JSON_ERROR',jsonTransformError:e?.name==='RangeError'?'RANGE_ERROR':'JSON_TRANSFORM_ERROR'};
 }
}
export const responseCopyPolicy='Finite ASCII screening; unknown/escaped/unicode forms and privacy absence unproved. With JSON redaction, parse/serialize can drop duplicate keys, reorder integer keys and normalize or lose numeric representation/precision. Indexed span offsets disclose lengths; marker metadata names actual replacements, but literal marker text can look identical. No-hit complete JSON retains exact bytes. Lone surrogates within a redacted string can become U+FFFD; unrelated strings retain their parsed representation. Marker counters may have harmless gaps after discarded traversal or key collisions. Byte fallback screening is weaker for embedded escaped tokens and cannot establish usable review acceptance. No complete/verbatim claim after transformation, withholding or capture/storage failure; no private raw store or original/span hashes after redaction.';
export function buildResponseArtifact(output){
 assert(Buffer.isBuffer(output.stdoutBuffer)&&Buffer.isBuffer(output.stderrBuffer),'RAW_RESPONSE_BUFFERS_REQUIRED');
 assert(output.stdoutBuffer.length<=8*1024*1024&&output.stderrBuffer.length<=1024*1024);
 let markerSequence=0;const nextMarker=kind=>'<REDACTED'+(kind==='KEY'?'_KEY':'')+':'+(markerSequence++)+'>';
 const incompleteReasons=[];
 if(output.timedOut===true)incompleteReasons.push('TIMEOUT');
 if(output.childError)incompleteReasons.push('CHILD_ERROR');
 if(output.closeObserved!==true)incompleteReasons.push('CLOSE_NOT_OBSERVED');
 if(output.overflow===true)incompleteReasons.push('OVERFLOW_TERMINATION');
 if(output.signal!=null)incompleteReasons.push('SIGNAL_TERMINATION');
 const make=(b,observedBytes,truncated,jsonEnvelope)=>{
  if(truncated||incompleteReasons.length)return {base64:'',originalSha256:null,publicSha256:hash(Buffer.alloc(0)),originalRetainedBytes:b.length,publicRetainedBytes:0,observedBytes,truncated:!!truncated,redactedSpans:[],mode:truncated?'TRUNCATED_PREFIX_WITHHELD':'INCOMPLETE_STREAM_WITHHELD',coordinate:null,incompleteReasons:[...(truncated?['CAP_TRUNCATION']:[]),...incompleteReasons],normalizationApplied:false,originalHashWithheldReason:'Incomplete stream may contain a partial secret below finite pattern thresholds'};
  const r=(jsonEnvelope?redactJsonEnvelope(b,nextMarker):null)??redactResponseBytes(b,{escapeAware:true,markerFactory:()=>nextMarker('VALUE')});
  return {base64:r.publicBytes.toString('base64'),originalSha256:r.originalSha256,publicSha256:r.publicSha256,originalRetainedBytes:b.length,publicRetainedBytes:r.publicBytes.length,observedBytes,truncated:false,redactedSpans:r.spans,mode:r.mode,coordinate:r.coordinate,jsonTransformError:r.jsonTransformError??null,incompleteReasons:[],normalizationApplied:r.mode==='JSON_ENVELOPE'&&r.spans.length>0,originalHashWithheldReason:r.spans.length?'Original/span hash could confirm a low-entropy redacted value':null};
 };
 return {scope:'PUBLIC RESPONSE COPY; decoded JSON leaves or labelled byte fallback; incomplete streams withheld',stdout:make(output.stdoutBuffer,output.stdoutObservedBytes,output.stdoutTruncated,true),stderr:make(output.stderrBuffer,output.stderrObservedBytes,output.stderrTruncated,false),exitCode:output.exitCode,closeObserved:output.closeObserved,screenLimit:responseCopyPolicy,streamCompletenessLimit:'Timeout/child error/missing close/overflow/signal imply withheld streams; stdin error or kill count alone does not, so a closed early-EOF result remains inspectable. Not a receiver or process-tree proof.',receiverBytesProved:false,automaticRetry:false};
}

export function requestPacket({cli,args,cwd,env,packetBuffer,timeoutMs=900000,graceMs=1000,exitGraceMs=5000,stdoutLimit=8*1024*1024,stderrLimit=1024*1024,spawnImpl=spawn,onSpawnAttempt=()=>{}}){
 assert.equal(typeof cli,'string');assert(Array.isArray(args));assert(Buffer.isBuffer(packetBuffer));for(const n of [timeoutMs,graceMs,exitGraceMs,stdoutLimit,stderrLimit])assert(Number.isSafeInteger(n)&&n>0);
 return new Promise(resolve=>{
  const started=Date.now();let child,timer,grace,settled=false,stdout=Buffer.alloc(0),stderr=Buffer.alloc(0),stdoutObservedBytes=0,stderrObservedBytes=0;
  let timedOut=false,overflow=false,spawnAttempted=false,spawnObserved=false,spawnFailure=false,childError=null,killError=null,killAttempts=[],killAttemptCount=0,killAttemptsOmitted=0,exitObserved=false,closeObserved=false,exitCode=null,signal=null,stdinError=null,stdinFinished=false,sendException=null;
  function finish(reason){if(settled)return;settled=true;clearTimeout(timer);clearTimeout(grace);const writableFinished=child?.stdin?.writableFinished===true,stdinBytesQueuedOrWritten=child?.stdin?.bytesWritten??0;
   // Queued/written counter is diagnostic only. finish is sender stream completion;
   // neither proves delivery. Missing close, errors, overflow or timeout fail closed.
   const senderComplete=closeObserved&&spawnObserved&&!spawnFailure&&!childError&&!timedOut&&!overflow&&stdinError===null&&sendException===null&&stdinFinished&&writableFinished;
   const result={stdout:stdout.toString('utf8'),stderr:stderr.toString('utf8'),stdoutBuffer:Buffer.from(stdout),stderrBuffer:Buffer.from(stderr),stdoutObservedBytes,stderrObservedBytes,stdoutTruncated:stdoutObservedBytes>stdout.length,stderrTruncated:stderrObservedBytes>stderr.length,exitCode,signal,closed:closeObserved,closeObserved,exitObserved,spawnAttempted,spawnObserved,spawnFailure,childError,killError,killAttempts,killAttemptCount,killAttemptsOmitted,timedOut,overflow,stdinError,stdinFinished,writableFinished,stdinBytesQueuedOrWritten,sendException,senderComplete,settlementReason:reason,receiverBytesProved:false,wallMs:Date.now()-started};
   if(!closeObserved){child?.stdin?.destroy();child?.stdout?.destroy();child?.stderr?.destroy();child?.unref?.();}
   resolve(result);
  }
  function armGrace(reason,ms=graceMs){if(!grace)grace=setTimeout(()=>finish(reason),ms);}
  function kill(reason){let error=null;try{if(child?.kill()===false)error='KILL_RETURNED_FALSE';}catch(e){error=code(e);}killError=error;killAttemptCount++;/* Bound diagnostic history to64entries; omissions counted, last error explicit. */if(killAttempts.length<64)killAttempts.push({reason,error,elapsedMs:Date.now()-started});else killAttemptsOmitted++;armGrace(reason);}
  try{spawnAttempted=true;onSpawnAttempt();child=spawnImpl(cli,args,{cwd,env,windowsHide:true,shell:false,stdio:['pipe','pipe','pipe']});}catch(e){spawnFailure=true;childError=code(e);finish('SPAWN_THROW');return;}
  timer=setTimeout(()=>{timedOut=true;kill('TIMEOUT_GRACE_EXPIRED');},timeoutMs);
  child.on('spawn',()=>{spawnObserved=true;});
  child.on('error',e=>{childError=code(e);if(!spawnObserved)spawnFailure=true;kill('CHILD_ERROR_GRACE_EXPIRED');});
  child.on('exit',(c,s)=>{exitObserved=true;exitCode=c;signal=s;armGrace('EXIT_WITHOUT_CLOSE_GRACE_EXPIRED',exitGraceMs);});
  child.on('close',(c,s)=>{closeObserved=true;exitCode=c;signal=s;finish('CLOSE');});
  function collect(which,b){if(settled)return;const part=Buffer.from(b),limit=which==='stdout'?stdoutLimit:stderrLimit;if(which==='stdout'){stdoutObservedBytes+=part.length;stdout=Buffer.concat([stdout,part.subarray(0,Math.max(0,limit-stdout.length))]);if(stdoutObservedBytes>limit)overflow=true;}else{stderrObservedBytes+=part.length;stderr=Buffer.concat([stderr,part.subarray(0,Math.max(0,limit-stderr.length))]);if(stderrObservedBytes>limit)overflow=true;}if(overflow)kill('OVERFLOW_GRACE_EXPIRED');}
  try{child.stdin.on('error',e=>{stdinError={code:code(e)};kill('STDIN_ERROR_GRACE_EXPIRED');});child.stdin.on('finish',()=>{stdinFinished=true;});child.stdout.on('data',b=>collect('stdout',b));child.stderr.on('data',b=>collect('stderr',b));child.stdin.end(packetBuffer);}catch(e){sendException=code(e);kill('SEND_EXCEPTION_GRACE_EXPIRED');}
 });
}
export function classifyResponse(output,parsed){
 if(output.senderComplete!==true)return 'SENDER_INCOMPLETE';
 if(output.exitCode===0&&output.closed&&!output.timedOut&&!output.overflow&&!output.spawnFailure&&!output.childError&&parsed&&parsed.is_error!==true&&typeof parsed.result==='string'&&parsed.result.trim())return 'NORMAL_RESPONSE';
 // Never search normal model review text for usage/authentication words.
 const text=parsed?.is_error===true?String(parsed.result??'')+' '+JSON.stringify(parsed.errors??[])+' '+output.stderr:output.stderr;
 if(/(?:hit|usage|rate|subscription|session).{0,100}limit|quota|limit.{0,80}reset/is.test(text))return 'SUBSCRIPTION_OR_USAGE_LIMIT';
 if(/prompt.{0,50}(long|large)|context.{0,50}(limit|exceed)|too many tokens/is.test(text))return 'INPUT_CONTEXT_LIMIT';
 if(/(?:unknown|invalid|unsupported).{0,100}(?:option|argument|model)|model.{0,100}(?:not exist|not available|not have access)/is.test(text))return 'UNSUPPORTED_CLI_OR_MODEL';
 if(/authentication|log.?in|not authenticated|unauthorized|expired.{0,50}token|OAuth/is.test(text))return 'SUBSCRIPTION_AUTHENTICATION_REQUIRED';
 return 'CLI_FAILURE_NO_VERDICT';
}
// Content-free terminal projection; unknown fields remain null, never raw output.
export function projectSenderTerminalEvidence(output){
 if(!output)return null;const out={},booleanFields=['closed','closeObserved','exitObserved','spawnAttempted','spawnObserved','spawnFailure','timedOut','overflow','stdinFinished','writableFinished','senderComplete','stdoutTruncated','stderrTruncated'];
 for(const k of booleanFields)out[k]=typeof output[k]==='boolean'?output[k]:null;
 for(const k of ['exitCode','stdoutObservedBytes','stderrObservedBytes','stdinBytesQueuedOrWritten','killAttemptCount','killAttemptsOmitted','wallMs'])out[k]=Number.isSafeInteger(output[k])?output[k]:null;
 const allowedCodes=new Set(['EPIPE','ENOENT','EACCES','EPERM','ENOSPC','EIO','EBADF','EINVAL','ERR_ASSERTION','ERR_STREAM_DESTROYED','ERR_STREAM_WRITE_AFTER_END','UNSPECIFIED_ERROR','KILL_RETURNED_FALSE']);
 const safeCode=v=>v==null?null:allowedCodes.has(v)?v:'UNRECOGNIZED_CODE';
 out.childError=safeCode(output.childError);out.killError=safeCode(output.killError);out.sendException=safeCode(output.sendException);out.stdinError=output.stdinError?{code:safeCode(output.stdinError.code)}:null;
 out.signal=output.signal==null?null:/^SIG[A-Z0-9]{1,12}$/.test(output.signal)?output.signal:'UNRECOGNIZED_SIGNAL';
 const reasons=new Set(['SPAWN_THROW','CLOSE','EXIT_WITHOUT_CLOSE_GRACE_EXPIRED','TIMEOUT_GRACE_EXPIRED','OVERFLOW_GRACE_EXPIRED','STDIN_ERROR_GRACE_EXPIRED','CHILD_ERROR_GRACE_EXPIRED','SEND_EXCEPTION_GRACE_EXPIRED']);
 out.settlementReason=output.settlementReason==null?null:reasons.has(output.settlementReason)?output.settlementReason:'UNRECOGNIZED_SETTLEMENT';
 out.stdoutRetainedAtFailureBytes=Buffer.isBuffer(output.stdoutBuffer)?output.stdoutBuffer.length:null;out.stderrRetainedAtFailureBytes=Buffer.isBuffer(output.stderrBuffer)?output.stderrBuffer.length:null;
 out.projectionLimit='Observed state at projection time; no content/environment/error messages. Unknown codes/reasons are labelled, not copied. Does not prove receiver bytes or process-tree closure.';
 return out;
}
export function isPublicCaptureUsable(responsePin){return responsePin?.modes?.stdout==='JSON_ENVELOPE';}

export async function executePacketProcedure({packet,readPacket,validateInputs,beforeFinalSenderCheck=()=>{},writeDispatch,request,writeResponse,writeFailure}){
 assert.equal(typeof writeResponse,'function','PUBLIC_RESPONSE_WRITER_REQUIRED');
 validateInputs();const packetBuffer=admitPacketBuffer(readPacket(),packet);beforeFinalSenderCheck(packetBuffer);const packetBefore=verifyPacketCopies(packetBuffer,readPacket(),packet);
 const actualStartUtc=new Date().toISOString();let dispatchCompleted=false,requestAttempted=false,spawnAttempted=false,failedStep='WRITE_DISPATCH',lastOutput=null,lastArtifact=null,responsePersistenceState='NOT_ATTEMPTED';
 try{writeDispatch({actualStartUtc,packetBefore});dispatchCompleted=true;failedStep='REQUEST';requestAttempted=true;
  const output=await request(packetBuffer,{onSpawnAttempt:()=>{spawnAttempted=true;}});lastOutput=output;failedStep='BUILD_RESPONSE_COPY';responsePersistenceState='BUILDING_IN_MEMORY';const responseArtifact=buildResponseArtifact(output);lastArtifact=responseArtifact;failedStep='PERSIST_RESPONSE_COPY';responsePersistenceState='ATTEMPTED_UNCERTAIN_INSPECT';const responsePin=writeResponse(responseArtifact);responsePersistenceState='CALLBACK_RETURNED';output.stdoutBuffer=Buffer.from(responseArtifact.stdout.base64,'base64');output.stderrBuffer=Buffer.from(responseArtifact.stderr.base64,'base64');output.stdout=output.stdoutBuffer.toString('utf8');output.stderr=output.stderrBuffer.toString('utf8');output.responseOriginalHashes={stdout:responseArtifact.stdout.originalSha256,stderr:responseArtifact.stderr.originalSha256};output.responseRedactionCounts={stdout:responseArtifact.stdout.redactedSpans.length,stderr:responseArtifact.stderr.redactedSpans.length};let inputsUnchangedAfter=true,packetAfter=null;
  try{validateInputs();packetAfter=verifyPacketCopies(packetBuffer,readPacket(),packet);}catch{inputsUnchangedAfter=false;}
  return {completed:true,dispatched:true,actualStartUtc,actualEndUtc:new Date().toISOString(),packetBefore,packetAfter,inputsUnchangedAfter,output,responsePersistenceState,responsePin};
 }catch(e){const failure={classification:dispatchCompleted?'POST_DISPATCH_EXCEPTION':'DISPATCH_WRITE_EXCEPTION',failedStep,errorCode:code(e),responsePersistenceState,senderEvidence:projectSenderTerminalEvidence(lastOutput),publicResponseHashes:lastArtifact?{stdout:lastArtifact.stdout.publicSha256,stderr:lastArtifact.stderr.publicSha256}:null,originalResponseHashesWithheldOnFailure:true,dispatchCompleted,dispatchState:dispatchCompleted?'callback returned; inspect retained record':'write attempted; inspect actual dispatch before continuation',requestAttempted,spawnAttempted,spawnState:spawnAttempted?'attempt observed; terminal identity unknown':requestAttempted?'request attempted; spawn not observed, absence not proof':'request not entered by this procedure',actualStartUtc,actualEndUtc:new Date().toISOString(),packetBefore,senderBufferSha256:hash(packetBuffer),senderBytes:packetBuffer.length,receiverBytesProved:false,automaticRetry:false,failureJournalPersisted:false};
  try{writeFailure(failure);failure.failureJournalPersisted=true;}catch(writerError){failure.failureWriterErrorCode=code(writerError);failure.recovery='Failure storage uncertain; inspect dispatch and all retained output. No automatic retry.';}
  return {completed:false,dispatched:requestAttempted,failure};
 }

    const failure={partition:packet.partition,actualEndUtc:new Date().toISOString(),exitCode:output.exitCode,signal:output.signal,closed:output.closed,timedOut:output.timedOut,classification,responseType:parsed?.type??null,responseSubtype:parsed?.subtype??null,isError:parsed?.is_error??null,errorCount:Array.isArray(parsed?.errors)?parsed.errors.length:null,stdoutBytes:Buffer.byteLength(output.stdout),stderrBytes:Buffer.byteLength(output.stderr),stdoutSha256:hash(output.stdout),stderrSha256:hash(output.stderr),manifestSha256:manifestHash,packetSha256:packet.sha256,verdict:'none',nativeAuthority:false,automaticRetry:false};
    write(failurePath,failure);process.stdout.write(JSON.stringify(failure)+'\n');process.exitCode=1;break;
  }
  let unchangedAfter=true;try{unchanged();}catch{unchangedAfter=false;}
  const models=Object.keys(parsed.modelUsage??{});const lines=parsed.result.split(/\r?\n/).filter(line=>line.trim()!=='');
  const headers=lines.filter(line=>line.startsWith('## Verdict:'));
  const scope='## Scope: GSS FOUNDATION '+packet.partition;
  const usable=parsed.result.startsWith('## Verdict: '+lines[0].slice('## Verdict: '.length)+'\n'+scope+'\n')&&unchangedAfter&&models.length===1&&models[0]===MODEL&&headers.length===1&&['## Verdict: Review OK','## Verdict: Changes requested'].includes(lines[0])&&lines.filter(line=>line===scope).length===1;
  const usage=models.map(model=>({model,...Object.fromEntries(['inputTokens','outputTokens','cacheReadInputTokens','cacheCreationInputTokens','webSearchRequests'].filter(key=>typeof parsed.modelUsage[model][key]==='number').map(key=>[key,parsed.modelUsage[model][key]]))}));
  const record={actualStartUtc,actualEndUtc,recorderSha256,cliBinarySha256,date:new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10),partition:packet.partition,scope:packet.title,reviewer:'Claude Code',cliVersion:'2.1.299',transport:'installed subscription CLI; tools/MCP/hooks disabled; no session persistence; no paid-provider fallback',requestedModel:MODEL,effort:'high',resolvedModels:models,exitCode:output.exitCode,closeObserved:output.closed,isError:parsed.is_error??false,durationMs:parsed.duration_ms,wallMs:output.wallMs,manifestSha256:manifestHash,packetSha256:packet.sha256,packetBytes:packet.bytes,reviewUsable:usable,inputsUnchangedAfter:unchangedAfter,verdict:headers[0]??null,usageByModel:usage,estimatedCostUsd:parsed.total_cost_usd,costInterpretation:'CLI estimate, not a paid API charge or measured subscription quota debit',nativeAuthority:false,retryAuthorized:false,applicationAuthorized:false,phase2Authorized:false,productApproval:false,wholeFoundationAccepted:false,result:parsed.result};
  write(runPath,record);
  write(mdPath,`# GSS actual Foundation CR03 STATIC intake/design review — partition ${packet.partition}\n\nDate:${actualEndUtc.slice(0,10)}. Reviewer:Claude Code2.1.299; model(s):${models.join(', ')}; effort=high.\nExact packet SHA256:${packet.sha256}; ${packet.bytes} UTF-8 bytes.\nManifest SHA256:${manifestHash}.\nTools/MCP/hooks disabled; Foundation CR03 static intake/map/design review only; no native replay, target/private restore, pilot completion or owner gate grant.\nCapture: complete actual model result below, without paraphrase.\nUsable:${usable}; exact inputs unchanged after:${unchangedAfter}.\n\n---\n\n${parsed.result}\n`);
  process.stdout.write(JSON.stringify({event:'Claude review captured',partition:packet.partition,usable,verdict:record.verdict,durationMs:record.durationMs,models})+'\n');
  if(!usable){process.exitCode=1;break;}
}

before NONE; any control-integrity or persistence defect still takes precedence.

unasserted requester closure prevents terminal invocation; physical output/record uncertainty is INCOMPLETE instead. InputsUnchangedAfter
false is ALWAYS CONTENT_MISMATCH/REFUSED, across classifications, not silently
closed as NONE. Known nonNORMAL enum means exactly one of the closed existing
classification values; no raw arbitrary class is admitted.

USABLE requires classification=NORMAL_RESPONSE. UNUSABLE requires a non-null
closed classification and the complete row above, including NORMAL_RESPONSE
only for the listed complete usability refusals. classification=null is allowed
ONLY INCOMPLETE/REFUSED where classification is genuinely unavailable; no forced
value from missing/corrupt evidence. verdict may be null in complete classified
failure or malformed feedback; it never implies acceptance. Operation refusal
by a core/preparer before/without an actual run emits no terminal record.

Negatives register EACH complete UNUSABLE row with a wrong code/disposition,
including dispatch/no-request,postdispatch REQUEST/BUILD/PERSIST,copy/noMD versus
failure-withMD,normal model/header/scope refusal,SENDER_INCOMPLETE,transformed and
fallback. Add inputsUnchangedAfter=false wrongly closed,requested versus resolved
model confusion,classification=null USABLE/UNUSABLE,nonNORMAL USABLE,any run with
equal declared/DERIVED known nonNORMAL class⇒CONTRADICTORY_OUTPUTS/REFUSED;
unequal declared/DERIVED classes exclusively CONTENT_MISMATCH/REFUSED,and precedence
misplacements. Full64trace positives and every inconsistent trace negative remain.

## Shared strict trust-JSON parser (actual31 S2)

One pure parseTrustJson function in the same Stage1 render-policy/CONTEXT_RENDER_SET
owns decoding of EVERY JSON byte buffer used in trust, schema, envelope, pin,
projection or provenance computation. All core,entry,requester-verifier,actual
requester/requester-template,pilot-preparer and sender/helper callers use it,
with source wiring/composition tests; no caller first uses
JSON.parse on the complete untrusted buffer and loses duplicates. Legacy pretty
formatting is allowed; no canonical-whitespace retrofit of historical files.
Source byte hashes always refer to original owned bytes.

The parser validates UTF8 and complete standard JSON token grammar, tracks a
separate key set for EACH object scope, decodes string escape sequences exactly
(including Unicode escapes), and REFUSES any repeated DECODED key in that object
before it can be overwritten or used. The same key in distinct objects is legal;
case-sensitive different keys are not duplicates but still undergo closed-schema
validation. Nested objects/arrays,escaped quotes/backslashes and strings holding
JSON-looking text are parsed structurally, never by a regex key scan. The whole
buffer must be consumed; malformed/trailing bytes refuse. Scalars and numeric
semantics then undergo existing closed-schema/safe-integer checks. Object creation
preserves keys without prototype-mutating assignments; unknown keys still refuse.
A deliberate whole-buffer JSON.parse result may only be used AFTER a complete
matching strict lexical/duplicate-key pass, never as the duplicate check itself.
SOURCE must demonstrate parser/resource/error behaviour and finite limits; no
universal depth/memory/privacy proof or original-byte normalization is claimed.

Applies to authority activation/grant/resolution,all actual control/proposal/config/
policy kinds,synthetic cases,map/rubric/key/assessment,manifest/dispatch/run/failure/
public-copy and all JSON primary provenance inputs,including a decoded public JSON
envelope used for source trust. Historical unavailable legacy original stdout
cannot be reparsed; its N5/derived-unscreened limits remain unchanged. Embedded
source-code strings are DATA, never interpreted as a new JSON control record.
Duplicate keys refuse UNSUPPORTED_SCHEMA: an operation-refusal code at prewrite
callers; REFUSED only if independently observed during actual terminal mode.

Register a duplicate-key negative per trust-relevant schema kind and each proposal/
case/authority kind,including nested duplicates and escaped aliases a versus
\u0061. A shadowed scope/generation/model/hash/recipe field must never reach envelope
or equality computation. Positive distinct-object equal keys and legitimate
pretty legacy records prove the parser does not reject them merely for formatting.
Every A1 construction rechecks the retained pilot assessment/grant with this same
parser/shared owner verifier; omitted later-generation check is a negative.

## Deterministic outcome predicates and total code orders (actual32 R2)

111101 is NORMAL_RESPONSE ONLY, matching source-reviewed requester failure/run
branches. A run with equal DECLARED and DERIVED known nonNORMAL class is
CONTRADICTORY_OUTPUTS/REFUSED (no normal closing row). Any DECLARED-versus-DERIVED
class mismatch is the earlier CONTENT_MISMATCH predicate,including NORMAL declared
but nonNORMAL derived. SENDER_INCOMPLETE and other complete nonNORMAL feedback use the matching
failure111010/111011 traces; missing/uncertain output remains INCOMPLETE.

After incomplete/contradictory/control-integrity checks pass, closed-unusable
predicates are INDEPENDENT: fallback mode,transformed SOURCE/PROCEDURE,resolved-
model mismatch,feedback header/scope/partition mismatch. Their combinations are
legal and never become REFUSED merely because two usability checks fail. Pick
COPY_REFUSED if fallback predicate holds; else TRANSFORMED_SOURCE if protected
SOURCE/PROCEDURE transformed predicate holds; else NONE for any model/header
feedback predicate. If none holds and all usable conditions pass,NONE/USABLE.
Thus transformed+badheader→TRANSFORMED_SOURCE;wrongmodel+badheader→NONE;
fallback+wrongmodel→COPY_REFUSED. Test every pair and all larger combinations.
Requested-model/authority/input/record-integrity defects are NOT usability flags.

The exhaustive predicate algorithm below supersedes the old unqualified
INCOMPLETE-first shorthand. Its explicit phases determine applicability; within
the REFUSED phase use exact order UNSUPPORTED_SCHEMA > STARTUP_REFUSED >
IDENTITY_MISMATCH > AUTHORITY_MISMATCH > CONTENT_MISMATCH > FOREIGN_OUTPUT >
CONTRADICTORY_OUTPUTS. Collect only causes proved by
complete records at that phase. No invented cause,lexical sorting or caller
preference. A complete duplicate-key finding is UNSUPPORTED_SCHEMA,not partial
bytes. Syntax-truncated records follow the specified legal/illegal phase order.
Test each simultaneous pair and larger set yields the first actual applicable code.
Operation-refusal uses same spelling but emits no terminal record before/without
entry terminal mode. These are deterministic classification rules,not new grants.

## Strict RAW stdout callers, metadata and placement cost (actual32 S2)

Requester/requester-template invokes parseTrustJson for bound manifest/admission/
all JSON trust inputs. Sender/helper redactJsonEnvelope and parsePublicResponse
perform this same strict pass on the ORIGINAL complete retained stdout JSON
buffer BEFORE JSON.parse/redaction/normalization or using result/model fields.
Incomplete/truncated streams remain withheld under existing capture policy;
they cannot claim raw duplicate-freedom. No duplicate-shadowed value may first
be used then normalized into an apparently valid public copy.

On raw duplicate-key refusal, do not parse a trusted result. Retain only the
privacy-policy public byte fallback/copy when safe,content-free strictParseEvidence
{stage:RAW_STDOUT_BEFORE_REDACTION,status:DUPLICATE_REFUSED,
parserPin:{path,sha256,bytes},code:UNSUPPORTED_SCHEMA}. No raw/key/value/span echo
or original/span hash after redaction/withholding. Checked complete no-duplicate
raw envelope records status:PASS at that stage; malformed/incomplete has explicit
MALFORMED_OR_INCOMPLETE status,neverPASS. All metadata is self-attested source
evidence,not raw-byte authenticity or hostile-executor proof. Normalized copies
CANNOT prove raw duplicate-freedom; core/provenance rechecks the actual strict
pass metadata/parser SOURCE identity and declared capture before trust. No
retroactive raw-pass assertion for unavailable legacy original stdout.

Complete raw-duplicate evidence produces UNSUPPORTED_SCHEMA/REFUSED in actual
entry terminal,even if ordinary classification is CLI_FAILURE_NO_VERDICT or
POST_DISPATCH_EXCEPTION. Incomplete/uncertain capture still follows INCOMPLETE
priority. The helper/requester operation refusal itself does not emit a terminal.
SOURCE trust requires a PASS from exact admitted parser/caller bytes where the
standard capture path claims it. Legacy initial SOURCE/PROCEDURE captures retain
the explicit DERIVED_UNSCREENED/legacy exception and limits,never a fabricated
raw duplicate-free or normalized-original identity proof.

parseTrustJson is frozen in CONTEXT_RENDER_SET alongside verifyOwnerEvidence
and verifyReviewProvenance. Any later repair of any of them or closure is the
explicit OWNER_BLOCK/new reviewed scope/pilot/grant cost; no silent non-context
helper/parser substitution. Their STANDARD branch behaviours remain SOURCE/
fictionally tested before activation; legacy pilot cannot execute those controls.
Register raw-stdout and requester-manifest duplicate negatives,escaped/nested
aliases,normalization concealing a duplicate,missingPASS metadata,wrong parser pin
and each per-kind schema duplicate. Existing resource/depth/privacy/race limits
remain; SOURCE must disclose concrete parser resource/error limits before use.

## Closed requester/CLI-child settlement predicates (actual35 R1)

requesterClosureAsserted:true is executor assertion that the original REQUESTER
cannot write own outputs anymore (or was never started/no launch in progress).
It does NOT assert CLI-child-tree termination. False/unknown assertion ⇒ operation
refusal/no terminal,owner run resolution remains required,no automatic launch.
The CLI child is the exact pinned installed subscription process with tools/MCP/
hooks disabled,no session persistence and no review-tool workspace writes. Under
that trusted CLI contract it cannot rewrite requester-owned BASE records after
the parent closes. A lingering child may still consume subscription resources;
child-tree termination and provider receiving bytes are NOT proved. We deliberately
permit a complete known failure to close UNUSABLE without child-close proof; no
replay/retry or source acceptance follows. Unexpected CLI workspace-write behaviour
violates this trusted assumption and blocks scope; it is not detected by lineage.

For NORMAL run,classification AND exception projections,STANDARD uses the SAME complete
closed senderEvidence projection defined below; not a seven-key minimum or the
legacy26-key classification object. Extra/missing keys/types refuse. Exception
senderEvidence may be null only before the owned BEFORE snapshot completed inside
REQUEST,as solely defined by the exception stage table (including construction
failure after output returned). CLASSIFICATION_FAILURE and NORMAL run require
non-null full projection. Legacy15 carriers
remain unadopted context and are not silently treated as the STANDARD schema.
Known finite error labels are observations,not child-termination proof.
Apply this EXACT consistency predicate before the closing failure table:
- CLOSE requires closeObserved=true; exitObserved/spawnObserved may be false for
known spawn-error closure. The exact senderComplete formula below applies to ALL
settlements,not only CLOSE,and intentionally excludes exitCode and signal.
- SPAWN_THROW requires closeObserved=false,spawnObserved=false,spawnFailure=true.
- EXIT_WITHOUT_CLOSE_GRACE_EXPIRED requires closeObserved=false,exitObserved=true.
- TIMEOUT_GRACE_EXPIRED requires closeObserved=false,timedOut=true.
- OVERFLOW_GRACE_EXPIRED requires closeObserved=false,overflow=true.
- STDIN_ERROR_GRACE_EXPIRED requires closeObserved=false,stdinError nonnull.
- CHILD_ERROR_GRACE_EXPIRED requires closeObserved=false,childError nonnull.
- SEND_EXCEPTION_GRACE_EXPIRED requires closeObserved=false,sendException nonnull.
Known enum but violated predicate⇒CONTRADICTORY_OUTPUTS/REFUSED; do not call it
INCOMPLETE. exitObserved/spawnObserved/killError/childError values not restricted
by the selected predicate are independently declared observations; they never
alone trigger phase1. killError success/failure is not child-termination proof.
Each consistent known nonNORMAL classification with complete matching C/F/MD-if-
result closes NONE/UNUSABLE even with closeObserved=false; physical uncertainty
or a higher complete control defect still wins the existing phase/enum order.

Exception failures have senderEvidence:null only at WRITE_DISPATCH or REQUEST
before an owned sender snapshot completed; REQUEST includes snapshot construction
failure after output returns. BUILD/PERSIST require the complete owned snapshot. Complete closed stage/attempt booleans and requester
closure suffice for known exception rows; null child evidence is disclosed,not
phase1. When senderEvidence is present it uses the same finite schema/consistency
predicate. No exception result is trusted; its standard result is explicitly null.
NORMAL_RESPONSE uses the new full run.senderEvidence carrier defined below,with
senderComplete=true,closeObserved=true,exitObserved=true,spawnObserved=true,
spawnFailure=false,exitCode=0,signal:null,CLOSE settlement,all four error fields null,
timedOut=false,overflow=false. First check all declared-versus-derived fields:
any recomputation mismatch is CONTENT_MISMATCH. Only a complete matching NORMAL
combination that still fails a normal-row fact is CONTRADICTORY_OUTPUTS; a separate
sourceRun flag mismatch keeps the earlier CONTENT_MISMATCH. Header/model-only
usability checks never bypass these control predicates.

## Standard exception/copy canonical fields (actual35 S1/S2)

STANDARD failure schema is an explicit union distinguished by failureKind enum

journal if the callback throws. Callback receives exactly those owned bytes and
must write/readback them; no second serialization/record mutation. Successful
responsePin matches them too. If serialization fails before a complete buffer,
expectedPin:null and no copy write has been attempted. A full observed C with null
expectedPin on an exception is a known illegal table combination: CONTENT_MISMATCH; known
hash/count mismatch⇒CONTENT_MISMATCH. Prefix comparison uses canonical own JSON;
exception C present requires rootstrictEvidence equal to copy.root. No original
secret response or span hashes are retained in this public expected pin.

Canonical own JSON comparison is compulsory for ALL standard own role JSON,not
just when an external expected hash exists. Strict parse preserves needed key
order under the declared JSON writer; reserialization includes the final LF.
Parseable final-LF truncation is a strict proper prefix⇒PARTIAL_RECORD on a legal
trace. Other complete noncanonical byte differences⇒CONTENT_MISMATCH. Recorded
whole expected hashes additionally bind what was intended,not just the parsed
current value. Canonicalization does not make forged-but-consistent values true.

## Complete STANDARD sender projection and transport/classification binding (actual36 R1/S1)

One complete closed senderEvidence schema is shared by NORMAL run,classification
failure and exception projection. Required boolean keys (all real observed booleans when an
output exists): closed,closeObserved,exitObserved,spawnAttempted,spawnObserved,
spawnFailure,timedOut,overflow,stdinFinished,writableFinished,senderComplete,
stdoutTruncated,stderrTruncated. closed MUST equal closeObserved; a complete known
boolean disagreement is exclusively CONTENT_MISMATCH,not CONTRADICTORY_OUTPUTS.
Required nullable safe-integer keys: exitCode,stdoutObservedBytes,stderrObservedBytes,
stdinBytesQueuedOrWritten,killAttemptCount,killAttemptsOmitted,wallMs,
stdoutRetainedAtProjectionBytes,stderrRetainedAtProjectionBytes. Counts/lengths/
duration nonnegative; exitCode is signed safe integer|null. exitObserved records
the exit EVENT,whereas close may also supply exitCode; nonnull code therefore does
not require exitObserved=true. Null is unknown,not zero. Required nullable error
keys childError,killError,sendException,stdinError are ALL null or exact {code}.
Normalize legacy string codes into that object; no mixed string/object carriers.
The closed STANDARD code set is EPIPE,ENOENT,EACCES,EPERM,ENOSPC,EIO,EBADF,EINVAL,
ERR_ASSERTION,ERR_STREAM_DESTROYED,ERR_STREAM_WRITE_AFTER_END,UNSPECIFIED_ERROR,
KILL_RETURNED_FALSE,EOF,ECONNRESET,UNRECOGNIZED_CODE. Unknown raw codes map to the
explicit UNRECOGNIZED_CODE; no raw message/environment/buffer. Required signal is
null,a string matching exactly /^SIG[A-Z0-9]{1,12}$/,or UNRECOGNIZED_SIGNAL.
Required settlementReason is SPAWN_THROW,CLOSE,EXIT_WITHOUT_CLOSE_GRACE_EXPIRED,
TIMEOUT_GRACE_EXPIRED,OVERFLOW_GRACE_EXPIRED,STDIN_ERROR_GRACE_EXPIRED,
CHILD_ERROR_GRACE_EXPIRED,SEND_EXCEPTION_GRACE_EXPIRED,or UNRECOGNIZED_SETTLEMENT.
Required projectionStage is BEFORE_PUBLIC_RESPONSE_MUTATION or AFTER_PUBLIC_RESPONSE_MUTATION.

// Exact task-owned fictional email permitted in packet and result. No account grant.
export const permittedPublicLiterals=Object.freeze(['fictional@example.invalid']);
// Original policy literals retained; JWT/EMAIL use explicit cursor-aware iterators.
const patterns=[['ANTHROPIC_KEY',/sk-ant-[A-Za-z0-9_-]{20,}/g],['API_KEY',/sk-(?:proj-)?[A-Za-z0-9_-]{40,}/g],['JWT',/eyJ[A-Za-z0-9_-]{12,}\.[A-Za-z0-9_-]{12,}\.[A-Za-z0-9_-]{12,}/g],['PRIVATE_KEY_HEADER',/-----BEGIN (?:RSA |EC |OPENSSH )?PRIVATE KEY-----/g],['EMAIL',/[A-Za-z0-9._%+-]+@[A-Za-z0-9.-]+\.[A-Za-z]{2,}/g],['AUTH_URL_CODE',/[?&](?:access_token|refresh_token|code)=[A-Za-z0-9_-]{12,}/gi]];
function* emailMatches(text){
 const boundary=/(?<![A-Za-z0-9._%+-])[A-Za-z0-9._%+-]+@[A-Za-z0-9.-]+\.[A-Za-z]{2,}/g;
 const sticky=/[A-Za-z0-9._%+-]+@[A-Za-z0-9.-]+\.[A-Za-z]{2,}/y;let cursor=0;
 while(cursor<text.length){
  let match=null;
  // A prior TLD may end mid-run. One sticky attempt preserves original global resume.
  if(cursor>0&&/[A-Za-z0-9._%+-]/.test(text[cursor-1])){sticky.lastIndex=cursor;match=sticky.exec(text);}
  if(!match){boundary.lastIndex=cursor;match=boundary.exec(text);}
  if(!match)break;yield{index:match.index,length:match[0].length};cursor=match.index+match[0].length;
 }
}
const jwtChar=n=>(n>=65&&n<=90)||(n>=97&&n<=122)||(n>=48&&n<=57)||n===95||n===45;
function* jwtMatches(text){
 let first=null,second=null,at=0,afterMatch=0;
 while(at<=text.length){
  const start=at;while(at<text.length&&jwtChar(text.charCodeAt(at)))at++;
  const third={start,end:at};
  if(first&&second&&second.end-second.start>=12&&third.end-third.start>=12){
   const from=Math.max(first.start,afterMatch);
   if(first.end-from>=15){
    // Search only this disjoint segment, leaving12 required characters after eyJ.
    const found=text.slice(from,first.end-12).indexOf('eyJ');
    if(found>=0){const index=from+found;yield{index,length:third.end-index};afterMatch=third.end;}
   }
  }
  first=second;second=third;
  if(at===text.length)break;
  if(text.charCodeAt(at)===46){at++;continue;}
  first=null;second=null;at++;
 }
}
function* regexMatches(re,text){re.lastIndex=0;for(const m of text.matchAll(re))yield{index:m.index,length:m[0].length};}
export function sensitiveSpans(bytes,{escapeAware=true}={}){
 assert(Buffer.isBuffer(bytes));const text=bytes.toString('latin1'),found=[];
 for(const [patternId,re]of patterns){for(const m of patternId==='JWT'?jwtMatches(text):patternId==='EMAIL'?emailMatches(text):regexMatches(re,text)){
  let start=m.index;
  if(escapeAware&&start>0&&text[start-1]==='\\'){
   let n=0;for(let i=start-1;i>=0&&text[i]==='\\';i--)n++;
   if(n%2===1){if(/^u[0-9A-Fa-f]{4}/.test(text.slice(start,start+5)))start+=5;else if('ntrbf'.includes(text[start]))start++;}
  }
  const end=m.index+m.length;if(start>=end)continue;
  if(patternId==='EMAIL'&&permittedPublicLiterals.includes(text.slice(start,end)))continue;
  found.push({startByte:start,endByte:end,patternIds:[patternId]});
 }}
 found.sort((a,b)=>a.startByte-b.startByte||a.endByte-b.endByte);const merged=[];
 for(const s of found){const last=merged.at(-1);if(last&&s.startByte<last.endByte){last.endByte=Math.max(last.endByte,s.endByte);last.patternIds=[...new Set([...last.patternIds,...s.patternIds])];}else merged.push({...s});}
 // No original span hash: low-entropy value confirmation is deliberately withheld.
 return merged;
}
export function redactResponseBytes(bytes,{markerFactory=i=>'<REDACTED:'+i+'>',...options}={}){
 const spans=sensitiveSpans(bytes,options),parts=[];let at=0;
 const publicSpans=spans.map((s,i)=>({...s,marker:markerFactory(i)}));
 for(const s of publicSpans){parts.push(bytes.subarray(at,s.startByte),Buffer.from(s.marker));at=s.endByte;}parts.push(bytes.subarray(at));
 const publicBytes=Buffer.concat(parts);return {publicBytes,spans:publicSpans,originalSha256:spans.length?null:hash(bytes),publicSha256:hash(publicBytes),originalRetainedBytes:bytes.length,mode:'BYTE_FALLBACK',coordinate:'ORIGINAL_RETAINED_BYTE_OFFSETS'};
}
function redactJsonEnvelope(bytes,nextMarker){
 let value;try{value=JSON.parse(new TextDecoder('utf8',{fatal:true,ignoreBOM:true}).decode(bytes));}catch{return null;}
 try{
  const spans=[];
  function string(s,entryPath){
   const r=redactResponseBytes(Buffer.from(s),{escapeAware:false,markerFactory:()=>nextMarker('VALUE')});
   for(const span of r.spans)spans.push({...span,entryPath,kind:'VALUE',coordinate:'DECODED_STRING_UTF8_BYTE_OFFSETS'});
   // Preserve unrelated strings, including lone surrogates, in the parsed value.
   return r.spans.length?r.publicBytes.toString('utf8'):s;
  }
  function visit(v,at){
   if(typeof v==='string')return string(v,at);
   if(Array.isArray(v))return v.map((x,i)=>visit(x,at+'[array:'+i+']'));
   if(v&&typeof v==='object'){
    const keys=Object.keys(v),reserved=new Set(keys),out=Object.create(null);
    for(const [i,k]of keys.entries()){
     const entryPath=at+'[object-entry:'+i+']',keySpans=sensitiveSpans(Buffer.from(k),{escapeAware:false});let publicKey=k;
     if(keySpans.length){do{publicKey=nextMarker('KEY');}while(reserved.has(publicKey)||Object.hasOwn(out,publicKey));
      for(const span of keySpans)spans.push({...span,marker:publicKey,entryPath,kind:'KEY',coordinate:'DECODED_STRING_UTF8_BYTE_OFFSETS'});
     }
     out[publicKey]=visit(v[k],entryPath+'.value');
    }return out;
   }return v;
  }
  const publicValue=visit(value,'$'),publicBytes=spans.length?Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(publicValue)):Buffer.from(bytes);
  return {publicBytes,spans,originalSha256:spans.length?null:hash(bytes),publicSha256:hash(publicBytes),originalRetainedBytes:bytes.length,mode:'JSON_ENVELOPE',coordinate:'DECODED_STRING_UTF8_BYTE_OFFSETS'};
 }catch(e){
  const r=redactResponseBytes(bytes,{escapeAware:true,markerFactory:()=>nextMarker('VALUE')});
  return {...r,mode:'BYTE_FALLBACK_AFTER_JSON_ERROR',jsonTransformError:e?.name==='RangeError'?'RANGE_ERROR':'JSON_TRANSFORM_ERROR'};
 }
}
export const responseCopyPolicy='Finite ASCII screening; unknown/escaped/unicode forms and privacy absence unproved. With JSON redaction, parse/serialize can drop duplicate keys, reorder integer keys and normalize or lose numeric representation/precision. Indexed span offsets disclose lengths; marker metadata names actual replacements, but literal marker text can look identical. No-hit complete JSON retains exact bytes. Lone surrogates within a redacted string can become U+FFFD; unrelated strings retain their parsed representation. Marker counters may have harmless gaps after discarded traversal or key collisions. Byte fallback screening is weaker for embedded escaped tokens and cannot establish usable review acceptance. No complete/verbatim claim after transformation, withholding or capture/storage failure; no private raw store or original/span hashes after redaction.';
export function buildResponseArtifact(output){
 assert(Buffer.isBuffer(output.stdoutBuffer)&&Buffer.isBuffer(output.stderrBuffer),'RAW_RESPONSE_BUFFERS_REQUIRED');
 assert(output.stdoutBuffer.length<=8*1024*1024&&output.stderrBuffer.length<=1024*1024);
 let markerSequence=0;const nextMarker=kind=>'<REDACTED'+(kind==='KEY'?'_KEY':'')+':'+(markerSequence++)+'>';
 const incompleteReasons=[];
 if(output.timedOut===true)incompleteReasons.push('TIMEOUT');
 if(output.childError)incompleteReasons.push('CHILD_ERROR');
 if(output.closeObserved!==true)incompleteReasons.push('CLOSE_NOT_OBSERVED');
 if(output.overflow===true)incompleteReasons.push('OVERFLOW_TERMINATION');
 if(output.signal!=null)incompleteReasons.push('SIGNAL_TERMINATION');
 const make=(b,observedBytes,truncated,jsonEnvelope)=>{
  if(truncated||incompleteReasons.length)return {base64:'',originalSha256:null,publicSha256:hash(Buffer.alloc(0)),originalRetainedBytes:b.length,publicRetainedBytes:0,observedBytes,truncated:!!truncated,redactedSpans:[],mode:truncated?'TRUNCATED_PREFIX_WITHHELD':'INCOMPLETE_STREAM_WITHHELD',coordinate:null,incompleteReasons:[...(truncated?['CAP_TRUNCATION']:[]),...incompleteReasons],normalizationApplied:false,originalHashWithheldReason:'Incomplete stream may contain a partial secret below finite pattern thresholds'};
  const r=(jsonEnvelope?redactJsonEnvelope(b,nextMarker):null)??redactResponseBytes(b,{escapeAware:true,markerFactory:()=>nextMarker('VALUE')});
  return {base64:r.publicBytes.toString('base64'),originalSha256:r.originalSha256,publicSha256:r.publicSha256,originalRetainedBytes:b.length,publicRetainedBytes:r.publicBytes.length,observedBytes,truncated:false,redactedSpans:r.spans,mode:r.mode,coordinate:r.coordinate,jsonTransformError:r.jsonTransformError??null,incompleteReasons:[],normalizationApplied:r.mode==='JSON_ENVELOPE'&&r.spans.length>0,originalHashWithheldReason:r.spans.length?'Original/span hash could confirm a low-entropy redacted value':null};
 };
 return {scope:'PUBLIC RESPONSE COPY; decoded JSON leaves or labelled byte fallback; incomplete streams withheld',stdout:make(output.stdoutBuffer,output.stdoutObservedBytes,output.stdoutTruncated,true),stderr:make(output.stderrBuffer,output.stderrObservedBytes,output.stderrTruncated,false),exitCode:output.exitCode,closeObserved:output.closeObserved,screenLimit:responseCopyPolicy,streamCompletenessLimit:'Timeout/child error/missing close/overflow/signal imply withheld streams; stdin error or kill count alone does not, so a closed early-EOF result remains inspectable. Not a receiver or process-tree proof.',receiverBytesProved:false,automaticRetry:false};
}
export function readRequesterPublic(root,p){
 if(typeof p!=='string'||path.isAbsolute(p)||p.includes('\\')||p.split('/').some(s=>['..','.','.local','.temp','.git','.aws','.env'].includes(s.toLowerCase())))throw Error('PUBLIC_PATH_REFUSED');
 const a=path.join(root,...p.split('/'));if(fs.realpathSync.native(a)!==a)throw Error('PUBLIC_REALPATH_REFUSED');const st=fs.lstatSync(a,{bigint:true});if(!st.isFile()||st.isSymbolicLink()||st.nlink!==1n)throw Error('PUBLIC_FILE_REFUSED');return fs.readFileSync(a);
}
export function parsePublicResponse(output){try{return JSON.parse(new TextDecoder('utf-8',{fatal:true,ignoreBOM:true}).decode(output.stdoutBuffer));}catch{return null;}}
export function assertRequesterPin({manifestPin,modulePath,moduleRelative,argvPath,bytes}){assert(manifestPin&&Buffer.isBuffer(bytes));assert.equal(argvPath,modulePath,'EXECUTING_ARGV_MODULE_MISMATCH');assert.equal(moduleRelative,manifestPin.path,'EXECUTING_MANIFEST_PATH_MISMATCH');assert.equal(hash(bytes),manifestPin.sha256,'EXECUTING_REQUESTER_HASH_MISMATCH');return {path:moduleRelative,sha256:hash(bytes),atomicExecutingBytesProved:false};}
export function assertRequesterStartup({execArgv,nodeOptionsPresent}){
 assert(Array.isArray(execArgv));assert.equal(execArgv.length,0,'STARTUP_EXECARGV_REFUSED');assert.equal(nodeOptionsPresent,false,'STARTUP_NODE_OPTIONS_REFUSED');
 return {execArgv:[],nodeOptionsPresent:false,startupChecked:true,precheckInjectedExecutionPrevented:false};
}
export function assertRuntimeRequesterIdentity({manifestPin,moduleUrl,argvPath,root,readPublic,startup}){
 const startupState=assertRequesterStartup(startup),modulePath=fileURLToPath(moduleUrl);
 assert.equal(argvPath,modulePath,'EXECUTING_ARGV_MODULE_MISMATCH');
 const moduleRelative=path.relative(root,modulePath).replaceAll('\\','/');
 return {...assertRequesterPin({manifestPin,modulePath,moduleRelative,argvPath,bytes:readPublic(moduleRelative)}),startup:startupState};
}
export function assertReviewedAdoptionBindings({reviewed,reviewedSha256,adopted,base,adoptedPath,manifestPin,dispatchSha256,runSha256,dispatchPath,runPath,existing=[]}){
 assert.equal(hash(reviewed),reviewedSha256,'REVIEWED_TEMPLATE_HASH_MISMATCH');assertAdoptionSource(reviewed,adopted,base,existing);assert.equal(dispatchPath,adoptedPath,'DISPATCH_REQUESTER_PATH_MISMATCH');assert.equal(runPath,adoptedPath,'RUN_REQUESTER_PATH_MISMATCH');
 const independentlyDerivedSha256=hash(adopted);assert.equal(manifestPin.path,adoptedPath);for(const value of [manifestPin.sha256,dispatchSha256,runSha256])assert.equal(value,independentlyDerivedSha256,'ADOPTED_TERMINAL_BINDING_MISMATCH');
 return {independentlyDerivedSha256,reviewedTemplateSha256:reviewedSha256,atomicExecutingBytesProved:false};
}

export function requestPacket({cli,args,cwd,env,packetBuffer,timeoutMs=900000,graceMs=1000,exitGraceMs=5000,stdoutLimit=8*1024*1024,stderrLimit=1024*1024,spawnImpl=spawn,onSpawnAttempt=()=>{}}){
 assert.equal(typeof cli,'string');assert(Array.isArray(args));assert(Buffer.isBuffer(packetBuffer));for(const n of [timeoutMs,graceMs,exitGraceMs,stdoutLimit,stderrLimit])assert(Number.isSafeInteger(n)&&n>0);
 return new Promise(resolve=>{
  const started=Date.now();let child,timer,grace,settled=false,stdoutObservedBytes=0,stderrObservedBytes=0;
  // Lazy private64KiB pages avoid re-copying the whole retained prefix per event.
  const streams={stdout:{pages:[],length:0,limit:stdoutLimit},stderr:{pages:[],length:0,limit:stderrLimit}};
  function retain(stream,part){
   const count=Math.min(part.length,Math.max(0,stream.limit-stream.length));let offset=0;
   while(offset<count){
    let page=stream.pages.at(-1);
    if(!page||page.used===page.bytes.length){page={bytes:Buffer.allocUnsafe(Math.min(65536,stream.limit-stream.length)),used:0};stream.pages.push(page);}
    const n=Math.min(count-offset,page.bytes.length-page.used);part.copy(page.bytes,page.used,offset,offset+n);page.used+=n;stream.length+=n;offset+=n;
   }
  }
  function retainedBytes(stream){return Buffer.concat(stream.pages.map(page=>page.bytes.subarray(0,page.used)),stream.length);}
  let timedOut=false,overflow=false,spawnAttempted=false,spawnObserved=false,spawnFailure=false,childError=null,killError=null,killAttempts=[],killAttemptCount=0,killAttemptsOmitted=0,exitObserved=false,closeObserved=false,exitCode=null,signal=null,stdinError=null,stdinFinished=false,sendException=null;
  function finish(reason){if(settled)return;settled=true;clearTimeout(timer);clearTimeout(grace);const writableFinished=child?.stdin?.writableFinished===true,stdinBytesQueuedOrWritten=child?.stdin?.bytesWritten??0;
   const stdout=retainedBytes(streams.stdout),stderr=retainedBytes(streams.stderr);
   // Queued/written counter is diagnostic only. finish is sender stream completion;
   // neither proves delivery. Missing close, errors, overflow or timeout fail closed.
   const senderComplete=closeObserved&&spawnObserved&&!spawnFailure&&!childError&&!timedOut&&!overflow&&stdinError===null&&sendException===null&&stdinFinished&&writableFinished;
   const result={stdout:stdout.toString('utf8'),stderr:stderr.toString('utf8'),stdoutBuffer:Buffer.from(stdout),stderrBuffer:Buffer.from(stderr),stdoutObservedBytes,stderrObservedBytes,stdoutTruncated:stdoutObservedBytes>stdout.length,stderrTruncated:stderrObservedBytes>stderr.length,exitCode,signal,closed:closeObserved,closeObserved,exitObserved,spawnAttempted,spawnObserved,spawnFailure,childError,killError,killAttempts,killAttemptCount,killAttemptsOmitted,timedOut,overflow,stdinError,stdinFinished,writableFinished,stdinBytesQueuedOrWritten,sendException,senderComplete,settlementReason:reason,receiverBytesProved:false,wallMs:Date.now()-started};
   if(!closeObserved){child?.stdin?.destroy();child?.stdout?.destroy();child?.stderr?.destroy();child?.unref?.();}
   resolve(result);
  }
  function armGrace(reason,ms=graceMs){if(!grace)grace=setTimeout(()=>finish(reason),ms);}
  function kill(reason){let error=null;try{if(child?.kill()===false)error='KILL_RETURNED_FALSE';}catch(e){error=code(e);}killError=error;killAttemptCount++;/* Bound diagnostic history to64entries; omissions counted, last error explicit. */if(killAttempts.length<64)killAttempts.push({reason,error,elapsedMs:Date.now()-started});else killAttemptsOmitted++;armGrace(reason);}
  try{spawnAttempted=true;onSpawnAttempt();child=spawnImpl(cli,args,{cwd,env,windowsHide:true,shell:false,stdio:['pipe','pipe','pipe']});}catch(e){spawnFailure=true;childError=code(e);finish('SPAWN_THROW');return;}
  timer=setTimeout(()=>{timedOut=true;kill('TIMEOUT_GRACE_EXPIRED');},timeoutMs);
  child.on('spawn',()=>{spawnObserved=true;});
  child.on('error',e=>{childError=code(e);if(!spawnObserved)spawnFailure=true;kill('CHILD_ERROR_GRACE_EXPIRED');});
  child.on('exit',(c,s)=>{exitObserved=true;exitCode=c;signal=s;armGrace('EXIT_WITHOUT_CLOSE_GRACE_EXPIRED',exitGraceMs);});
  child.on('close',(c,s)=>{closeObserved=true;exitCode=c;signal=s;finish('CLOSE');});
  function collect(which,b){if(settled)return;const part=Buffer.from(b),stream=streams[which];retain(stream,part);if(which==='stdout'){stdoutObservedBytes+=part.length;if(stdoutObservedBytes>stream.limit)overflow=true;}else{stderrObservedBytes+=part.length;if(stderrObservedBytes>stream.limit)overflow=true;}if(overflow)kill('OVERFLOW_GRACE_EXPIRED');}
  try{child.stdin.on('error',e=>{stdinError={code:code(e)};kill('STDIN_ERROR_GRACE_EXPIRED');});child.stdin.on('finish',()=>{stdinFinished=true;});child.stdout.on('data',b=>collect('stdout',b));child.stderr.on('data',b=>collect('stderr',b));child.stdin.end(packetBuffer);}catch(e){sendException=code(e);kill('SEND_EXCEPTION_GRACE_EXPIRED');}
 });
}
export function classifyResponse(output,parsed){
 if(output.senderComplete!==true)return 'SENDER_INCOMPLETE';
 if(output.exitCode===0&&output.closed&&!output.timedOut&&!output.overflow&&!output.spawnFailure&&!output.childError&&parsed&&parsed.is_error!==true&&typeof parsed.result==='string'&&parsed.result.trim())return 'NORMAL_RESPONSE';
 // Never search normal model review text for usage/authentication words.
 const text=parsed?.is_error===true?String(parsed.result??'')+' '+JSON.stringify(parsed.errors??[])+' '+output.stderr:output.stderr;
 if(/(?:hit|usage|rate|subscription|session).{0,100}limit|quota|limit.{0,80}reset/is.test(text))return 'SUBSCRIPTION_OR_USAGE_LIMIT';
 if(/prompt.{0,50}(long|large)|context.{0,50}(limit|exceed)|too many tokens/is.test(text))return 'INPUT_CONTEXT_LIMIT';
 if(/(?:unknown|invalid|unsupported).{0,100}(?:option|argument|model)|model.{0,100}(?:not exist|not available|not have access)/is.test(text))return 'UNSUPPORTED_CLI_OR_MODEL';
 if(/authentication|log.?in|not authenticated|unauthorized|expired.{0,50}token|OAuth/is.test(text))return 'SUBSCRIPTION_AUTHENTICATION_REQUIRED';
 return 'CLI_FAILURE_NO_VERDICT';
}
// One REQUEST capture owns every raw fact/buffer; callbacks never receive it.
const senderPublicCodes=new Set(['EPIPE','ENOENT','EACCES','EPERM','ENOSPC','EIO','EBADF','EINVAL','ERR_ASSERTION','ERR_STREAM_DESTROYED','ERR_STREAM_WRITE_AFTER_END','UNSPECIFIED_ERROR','KILL_RETURNED_FALSE','EOF','ECONNRESET','UNRECOGNIZED_CODE']);
const senderProjectionLimit='Observed state at projection time; no content/environment/error messages. Unknown codes/reasons are labelled, not copied. Does not prove receiver bytes or process-tree closure.';
const publicCode=value=>typeof value==='string'&&senderPublicCodes.has(value)?value:'UNRECOGNIZED_CODE';
function exceptionPublicCode(error){try{return publicCode(typeof error?.code==='string'?error.code:'UNSPECIFIED_ERROR');}catch{return 'UNRECOGNIZED_CODE';}}
const senderBooleans=Object.freeze(['closed','closeObserved','exitObserved','spawnAttempted','spawnObserved','spawnFailure','timedOut','overflow','stdinFinished','writableFinished','senderComplete','stdoutTruncated','stderrTruncated']);
const senderIntegers=Object.freeze(['exitCode','stdoutObservedBytes','stderrObservedBytes','stdinBytesQueuedOrWritten','killAttemptCount','killAttemptsOmitted','wallMs']);
const senderErrors=Object.freeze(['childError','killError','sendException','stdinError']);
const settlements=new Set(['SPAWN_THROW','CLOSE','EXIT_WITHOUT_CLOSE_GRACE_EXPIRED','TIMEOUT_GRACE_EXPIRED','OVERFLOW_GRACE_EXPIRED','STDIN_ERROR_GRACE_EXPIRED','CHILD_ERROR_GRACE_EXPIRED','SEND_EXCEPTION_GRACE_EXPIRED']);
const senderFactKeys=Object.freeze([...senderBooleans,...senderIntegers,...senderErrors,'signal','settlementReason']);
function captureSenderOutput(output){
 assert(output&&typeof output==='object'&&!Array.isArray(output),'SENDER_OUTPUT_REQUIRED');
 const owned={};
 for(const key of senderBooleans){
  assert(Object.hasOwn(output,key),'SENDER_BOOLEAN_FACT_REQUIRED:'+key);
  const value=output[key];assert.equal(typeof value,'boolean','SENDER_BOOLEAN_REQUIRED:'+key);owned[key]=value;
 }
 for(const key of senderIntegers){
  assert(Object.hasOwn(output,key),'SENDER_INTEGER_FACT_REQUIRED:'+key);
  const value=output[key];assert(value!==undefined,'SENDER_INTEGER_FACT_REQUIRED:'+key);
  assert(value===null||Number.isSafeInteger(value),'SENDER_INTEGER_TYPE_REFUSED:'+key);
  if(value!==null)assert(key==='exitCode'||value>=0,'NEGATIVE_SENDER_COUNT:'+key);owned[key]=value;
 }
 for(const key of senderErrors){
  assert(Object.hasOwn(output,key),'SENDER_ERROR_FACT_REQUIRED:'+key);
  const value=output[key];assert(value!==undefined,'SENDER_ERROR_FACT_REQUIRED:'+key);
  if(value===null){owned[key]=null;continue;}
  assert(typeof value==='string'||(typeof value==='object'&&!Array.isArray(value)),'SENDER_ERROR_TYPE_REFUSED:'+key);
  let errorCode=value;
  if(typeof value==='object'){assert(Object.hasOwn(value,'code'),'SENDER_ERROR_CODE_REQUIRED:'+key);errorCode=value.code;}
  assert.equal(typeof errorCode,'string','SENDER_ERROR_CODE_TYPE_REFUSED:'+key);
  owned[key]=Object.freeze({code:publicCode(errorCode)});
 }
 assert(Object.hasOwn(output,'signal'),'SENDER_SIGNAL_FACT_REQUIRED');
 const signal=output.signal;assert(signal!==undefined,'SENDER_SIGNAL_FACT_REQUIRED');assert(signal===null||typeof signal==='string','SENDER_SIGNAL_TYPE_REFUSED');
 owned.signal=signal===null?null:typeof signal==='string'&&/^SIG[A-Z0-9]{1,12}$/.test(signal)?signal:'UNRECOGNIZED_SIGNAL';
 assert(Object.hasOwn(output,'settlementReason'),'SENDER_SETTLEMENT_FACT_REQUIRED');
 const reason=output.settlementReason;assert.equal(typeof reason,'string','SENDER_SETTLEMENT_FACT_REQUIRED');
 owned.settlementReason=settlements.has(reason)?reason:'UNRECOGNIZED_SETTLEMENT';
 for(const key of ['stdoutBuffer','stderrBuffer']){
  assert(Object.hasOwn(output,key),'SENDER_BUFFER_FACT_REQUIRED');const buffer=output[key];
  assert(Buffer.isBuffer(buffer),'SENDER_BUFFER_TYPE_REFUSED');
  assert(buffer.length<=(key==='stdoutBuffer'?8*1024*1024:1024*1024),'SENDER_BUFFER_LIMIT_REFUSED');
  owned[key]=Buffer.from(buffer);
 }
 // Buffer contents cannot be Object.freeze'd. Ownership is by private copies,
 // no escape before public transformation; not a cross-getter atomic sample.
 assert.equal(owned.closed,owned.closeObserved,'SENDER_CLOSED_ALIAS_MISMATCH');
 assert.equal(owned.senderComplete,derivedSenderCompletion(owned),'SENDER_COMPLETION_MISMATCH');
 return Object.freeze(owned);
}
function projectCapturedEvidence(capture,projectionStage,stdout=capture.stdoutBuffer,stderr=capture.stderrBuffer){
 assert(['BEFORE_PUBLIC_RESPONSE_MUTATION','AFTER_PUBLIC_RESPONSE_MUTATION'].includes(projectionStage),'PROJECTION_STAGE_REQUIRED');
 const out={};for(const key of senderFactKeys)out[key]=capture[key];
 out.stdoutRetainedAtProjectionBytes=stdout.length;out.stderrRetainedAtProjectionBytes=stderr.length;
 out.projectionStage=projectionStage;out.projectionLimit=senderProjectionLimit;
 assert.equal(Object.keys(out).length,30);return Object.freeze(out);
}
export function projectSenderTerminalEvidence(output,projectionStage){
 assert(['BEFORE_PUBLIC_RESPONSE_MUTATION','AFTER_PUBLIC_RESPONSE_MUTATION'].includes(projectionStage),'PROJECTION_STAGE_REQUIRED');
 return projectCapturedEvidence(captureSenderOutput(output),projectionStage);
}
function derivedSenderCompletion(e){return e.closeObserved&&e.spawnObserved&&!e.spawnFailure&&e.childError===null&&!e.timedOut&&!e.overflow&&e.stdinError===null&&e.sendException===null&&e.stdinFinished&&e.writableFinished;}
export function isPublicCaptureUsable(responsePin){return responsePin?.modes?.stdout==='JSON_ENVELOPE';}
function callSync(name,callback,...args){
 assert.equal(typeof callback,'function','SYNC_CALLBACK_FUNCTION_REQUIRED:'+name);
 assert(!types.isAsyncFunction(callback),'SYNC_CALLBACK_REQUIRED:'+name);assert(!types.isGeneratorFunction(callback),'SYNC_CALLBACK_REQUIRED:'+name);
 const value=callback(...args);
 if(types.isPromise(value)){
  // Observe ordinary native rejection without a blanket unhandledRejection hook.
  // Exotic species/global-intrinsic behavior remains a controlled-caller obligation.
  Promise.prototype.then.call(value,undefined,()=>{});
  assert.fail('SYNC_CALLBACK_REQUIRED:'+name);
 }
 if(value!==null&&(typeof value==='object'||typeof value==='function')){
  assert(typeof value.then!=='function','SYNC_CALLBACK_REQUIRED:'+name);
 }
 if(['validateInputs','beforeFinalSenderCheck','writeDispatch','writeFailure'].includes(name))assert.equal(value,undefined,'VOID_CALLBACK_RETURN_REQUIRED:'+name);
 return value;
}
function freezePlain(value){
 if(value&&typeof value==='object'){for(const child of Object.values(value))freezePlain(child);Object.freeze(value);}return value;
}
function assertArtifactDate(value){
 assert(typeof value==='string'&&/^20[0-9]{6}$/.test(value),'ARTIFACT_DATE_REQUIRED');
 const year=Number(value.slice(0,4)),month=Number(value.slice(4,6)),day=Number(value.slice(6,8));
 const date=new Date(Date.UTC(year,month-1,day));
 assert(date.getUTCFullYear()===year&&date.getUTCMonth()+1===month&&date.getUTCDate()===day,'ARTIFACT_DATE_REQUIRED');
}
// Partial procedure only. The STANDARD caller still owns admitted BASE/date,
// strict parser/root/record binding, physical persistence and complete closure.
export async function executePacketProcedure({packet,readPacket,validateInputs,beforeFinalSenderCheck=()=>{},writeDispatch,request,writeResponse,writeFailure,base,artifactDate}){
 assert.equal(typeof writeResponse,'function','PUBLIC_RESPONSE_WRITER_REQUIRED');assert.equal(typeof writeFailure,'function','FAILURE_WRITER_REQUIRED');
 for(const [name,callback] of Object.entries({readPacket,validateInputs,beforeFinalSenderCheck,writeDispatch,writeResponse,writeFailure})){
  assert.equal(typeof callback,'function','SYNC_CALLBACK_FUNCTION_REQUIRED:'+name);assert(!types.isAsyncFunction(callback),'SYNC_CALLBACK_REQUIRED:'+name);assert(!types.isGeneratorFunction(callback),'SYNC_CALLBACK_REQUIRED:'+name);
 }
 assert.equal(typeof request,'function','REQUEST_FUNCTION_REQUIRED');assertReviewBase(base);assertArtifactDate(artifactDate);
 const copyPath=base+'_public-response-copy_'+artifactDate+'.json';
 // STANDARD caller MUST sanitize rejected pre-try errors; never persist raw messages/objects.
 // Reading a then property can invoke its getter; custom then itself is not called.
 callSync('validateInputs',validateInputs);const packetBuffer=admitPacketBuffer(callSync('readPacket',readPacket),packet);
 callSync('beforeFinalSenderCheck',beforeFinalSenderCheck,packetBuffer);const packetBefore=Object.freeze(verifyPacketCopies(packetBuffer,callSync('readPacket',readPacket),packet));
 const actualStartUtc=new Date().toISOString();let dispatchCompleted=false,requestAttempted=false,spawnAttempted=false,failedStep='WRITE_DISPATCH',senderBefore=null,lastArtifact=null,publicCopyExpectedPin=null,responsePersistenceState='NOT_ATTEMPTED';
 try{
  callSync('writeDispatch',writeDispatch,{actualStartUtc,packetBefore,base,dispatchPath:base+'_dispatch_'+artifactDate+'.json'});
  dispatchCompleted=true;failedStep='REQUEST';requestAttempted=true;
  const originalOutput=await request(packetBuffer,{onSpawnAttempt:()=>{spawnAttempted=true;}});
  const capture=captureSenderOutput(originalOutput);
  // Assignment only after complete capture/projection: no partial snapshot escapes.
  senderBefore=projectCapturedEvidence(capture,'BEFORE_PUBLIC_RESPONSE_MUTATION');
  failedStep='BUILD_RESPONSE_COPY';responsePersistenceState='BUILDING_IN_MEMORY';const responseArtifact=buildResponseArtifact(capture);lastArtifact=responseArtifact;
  const copyBytes=Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(responseArtifact,null,2)+'\n');
  // Whole serialized bytes are scanned, including base64 characters; decoded stream
  // content screening is buildResponseArtifact's earlier policy. No universal time bound.
  assert.equal(sensitiveSpans(copyBytes).length,0,'PUBLIC_COPY_SCREEN_REFUSED');
  publicCopyExpectedPin=Object.freeze({path:copyPath,sha256:hash(copyBytes),bytes:copyBytes.length});
  failedStep='PERSIST_RESPONSE_COPY';responsePersistenceState='ATTEMPTED_UNCERTAIN_INSPECT';
  const writerBytes=Buffer.from(copyBytes),returnedPin=callSync('writeResponse',writeResponse,writerBytes,publicCopyExpectedPin);
  responsePersistenceState='CALLBACK_RETURNED';
  assert(returnedPin&&typeof returnedPin==='object','RETURNED_RESPONSE_PIN_REQUIRED');
  const returned={};for(const key of ['path','sha256','bytes']){assert(Object.hasOwn(returnedPin,key),'RETURNED_RESPONSE_PIN_FIELD_REQUIRED:'+key);returned[key]=returnedPin[key];assert.equal(returned[key],publicCopyExpectedPin[key]);}
  // Check after pin getters too; the callback copy is the only writer-visible bytes.
  assert.equal(hash(writerBytes),publicCopyExpectedPin.sha256,'CALLBACK_COPY_BYTES_CHANGED');
  const artifactModes=Object.freeze({stdout:responseArtifact.stdout.mode,stderr:responseArtifact.stderr.mode});
  const responsePin=Object.freeze({...returned,modes:artifactModes});
  const stdoutBuffer=Buffer.from(responseArtifact.stdout.base64,'base64'),stderrBuffer=Buffer.from(responseArtifact.stderr.base64,'base64');
  const output=Object.freeze({...capture,stdoutBuffer,stderrBuffer,stdout:stdoutBuffer.toString('utf8'),stderr:stderrBuffer.toString('utf8'),responseOriginalHashes:Object.freeze({stdout:responseArtifact.stdout.originalSha256,stderr:responseArtifact.stderr.originalSha256}),responseRedactionCounts:Object.freeze({stdout:responseArtifact.stdout.redactedSpans.length,stderr:responseArtifact.stderr.redactedSpans.length})});
  const senderEvidence=projectCapturedEvidence(capture,'AFTER_PUBLIC_RESPONSE_MUTATION',stdoutBuffer,stderrBuffer),parsed=parsePublicResponse(output),classification=classifyResponse({...output,closed:senderEvidence.closeObserved,senderComplete:derivedSenderCompletion(senderEvidence)},parsed);
  let inputsUnchangedAfter=true,packetAfter=null;try{callSync('validateInputs',validateInputs);packetAfter=verifyPacketCopies(packetBuffer,callSync('readPacket',readPacket),packet);}catch{inputsUnchangedAfter=false;}
  return {completed:true,dispatched:true,actualStartUtc,actualEndUtc:new Date().toISOString(),packetBefore,packetAfter,inputsUnchangedAfter,output,senderEvidence,classification,responsePersistenceState,responsePin,publicCopyExpectedPin,artifactModes};
 }catch(error){
  const failure={failureKind:'EXCEPTION_FAILURE',classification:dispatchCompleted?'POST_DISPATCH_EXCEPTION':'DISPATCH_WRITE_EXCEPTION',failedStep,errorCode:exceptionPublicCode(error),result:null,responsePin:null,responsePersistenceState,senderEvidence:senderBefore,publicCopyExpectedPin,publicResponseHashes:lastArtifact?{stdout:lastArtifact.stdout.publicSha256,stderr:lastArtifact.stderr.publicSha256}:null,originalResponseHashesWithheldOnFailure:true,dispatchCompleted,dispatchState:dispatchCompleted?'callback returned; inspect retained record':'write attempted; inspect actual dispatch before continuation',requestAttempted,spawnAttempted,spawnState:spawnAttempted?'attempt observed; terminal identity unknown':requestAttempted?'request attempted; spawn not observed, absence not proof':'request not entered by this procedure',actualStartUtc,actualEndUtc:new Date().toISOString(),packetBefore,senderBufferSha256:hash(packetBuffer),senderBytes:packetBuffer.length,receiverBytesProved:false,automaticRetry:false,failureJournalPersisted:false};
  try{
   callSync('writeFailure',writeFailure,freezePlain(structuredClone(failure)));
   failure.failureJournalPersisted=true;
  }catch(writerError){failure.failureWriterErrorCode=exceptionPublicCode(writerError);failure.recovery='Failure storage uncertain; inspect dispatch and all retained output. No automatic retry.';}
  return {completed:false,dispatched:requestAttempted,failure};
 }
}

===== END BODY =====
