## Proposed fixed PC policy

RENDER_POLICY is the single CONTEXT_RENDER_SET owner of EVERY resource constant, fixed parse profile, finite-screen/classification policy, sender completion formula, artifact and transform correlation predicate used by provenance or typed schema. It exports pure reviewed functions and the closed immutable resourcePolicy value below. HELPER and TEMPLATE delegate to those same functions/constants; they have no authoritative mirrors, literal overrides or independent classifier/completion implementation. Pure provenance imports Node builtins only and obtains these definitions from its own owning RENDER_POLICY module, never from NON_CONTEXT code or the record under test. Helper/worker implementation changes are NON_CONTEXT only if all these CONTEXT bytes and semantics remain identical. ANY control/predicate change changes context; once anchored it requires OWNER_BLOCK/new scope/pilot/grant, not a helper-only successor. Every changed control or predicate versions policyId; source pins also bind exact bytes. Initial V4_PC remains unanchored and unapproved. Configuration is binding-only, no numeric slots. Test-only profiles are fictional and cannot become actual defaults. This deliberately accepts the larger future context-change cost (54B1 option b).

| Control | Candidate value | Purpose / justification |
|---|---:|---|
| Original retained stdout/stderr | 8388608 / 1048576 bytes | Preserve current stream caps and incomplete-stream policy. |
| Accepted finite pattern hits, cumulative both streams | 4096 | Bound allocation before collecting/merging matches; far above the four-result sample, not inferred universal sufficiency. |
| Metadata encoded-byte charge, cumulative | 1048576 | Bound long entry paths and per-hit metadata as well as row count. |
| One generated metadata entryPath | 2048 UTF-8 bytes | Refuse before constructing an arbitrarily long path/row. |
| Public decoded stdout/stderr | 12582912 / 2097152 bytes | Allow finite redaction/serialization growth while bounding later base64. |
| Complete canonical public copy | 25165824 bytes | Cover base64 of14MiB public bytes (~18.667MiB),1MiB metadata plus fixed fields, with margin; still enforce exact final bound. |
| Provider-envelope structural depth / value nodes | 128 / 100000 | Limit parsing/traversal before creating the full object graph; count every value and object key work. |
| Transformation worker wall budget | 15000ms | Well above earlier3.85s sample; scheduling observation, not a universal time guarantee. |
| Termination exit-observation grace | 5000ms | Bound parent waiting after termination request; missing exit is uncertain, not success. |
| Worker old-generation / stack | 256MiB / 4MiB | Additional local controls; neither bounds RSS/external buffers nor proves no OOM. |

The scanner counts every non-allowlisted finite-pattern hit after escape adjustment, before storing it and before overlap merging. Multiple pattern hits that later merge still consume quota; this conservative choice is explicit. Allowlisted matches still advance the exact global cursor but create no metadata. Reuse accepted24 EMAIL/JWT/escape semantics for admitted in-budget inputs; no shorter email grammar, skipped pattern, dropped context or truncated successful response. A quota failure aborts the complete transformation. Do not keep a prefix or silently omit span rows.

Exact closed policy keys/value (fixed order for generation; typed readers check every key/value, not a caller's relaxed duplicate):

    {"policyId":"GSS_CR03_RESPONSE_RESOURCES_V4_PC","originalStdoutBytes":8388608,"originalStderrBytes":1048576,"maxPatternHits":4096,"maxMetadataByteCharge":1048576,"maxEntryPathBytes":2048,"publicStdoutBytes":12582912,"publicStderrBytes":2097152,"maxCanonicalCopyBytes":25165824,"maxEnvelopeDepth":128,"maxEnvelopeNodes":100000,"workerDeadlineMs":15000,"terminationGraceMs":5000,"workerOldGenerationMiB":256,"workerStackMiB":4}

Use one per-transformation budget for stdout/stderr, JSON keys/values and byte fallback. Per-leaf resets would bypass the cumulative limit. The final serialized-copy screen has its own bounded hit counter and cannot consume or overwrite stream evidence; any hit retains the existing PUBLIC_COPY_SCREEN_REFUSED failure. It still scans whole serialized bytes; decoded stream policy runs earlier.

Before every path concatenation validate its resulting length; or generate paths lazily only for hits with bounded ancestor ordinals. No unbounded path is created for hit-free nodes. Metadata row strings MUST be generated ASCII without escapes (ordinal paths, markers and closed enums), never raw keys/values. Charge UTF8(JSON.stringify(row,null,2))+8bytes per line+2delimiters before appending; row shape/nesting is fixed and the charge overestimates canonical indentation. Counters span both streams and are checked before allocation. Public-byte budgets apply incrementally before base64; final JSON2+LF copy bytes are checked before Buffer allocation. Earlier limits bound the string construction too. Public-copy strict parse uses24MiB/depth64/200000value-nodes, with separate object-name accounting bounded by node count; test maximum4096rows split between both streams plus all fixed/rootstrict fields. This is a candidate ceiling, not evidence that all values complete under worker heap limits.

Candidate policy deliberately makes some otherwise complete large/dense responses unusable. No complete public feedback is available after such a refusal; the one-shot provider request remains consumed. No raw response is stored to recover it, and it is never automatically retried. This trade-off must be explicit in the reviewed procedure and owner-facing adoption decision. The current human optimization acceptance alone must not be relabelled as an exact numerical policy/role activation grant.

Existing requesterClosureAsserted remains a terminal invocation precondition: false/unknown refuses the operation without terminal; true asserts no requester writer remains and therefore its thread cannot persist output. A complete TRANSFORM_EXIT_UNCERTAIN BUILD failure can close unusable with the observation unchanged. No later worker observation/new terminal overwrite or retry path.
6. Add these exact phase6 closing rows to Design43, all presuming closure assertion and no earlier defect:

| trace / classification / stage / precise condition | reason | disposition |
|---|---|---|
| 110010 / POST_DISPATCH_EXCEPTION / JOURNAL_RESPONSE_TRANSFORM / NOT_ATTEMPTED with the complete correlation row; attempted physical phase files resolved absent or exact complete, no partial/uncertainty | NONE | UNUSABLE |

WRITE/REQUEST rows retain their old outcomes with journal/transform fields null. Journal-stage failure permits a complete start record after a throwing writer even though callback state remains START_ATTEMPTED_UNCERTAIN; primary resolution and expected pin must match. No worker starts in JOURNAL. Partial files/unknownphysical state remain phases1/3. Every complete no-row combination still CONTRADICTORY_OUTPUTS/REFUSED. Thus benign phase-write failures now have explicit usable mapping to UNUSABLE, not accidental REFUSED.

Expand tests to every64presence case cross absent/committed/uncertain/contradictory journal profiles and each new failure stage. It cannot reconstruct withheld original secret bytes or prove the worker actually executed from claims/timestamps; it validates the source-admitted protocol observations and public correlations. ## Normative58 clarification: BUILD observations and classification (54C1/C2)

The worker and parent BOTH screen the complete canonical copy, independently, using the CONTEXT finite policy and separate bounded screening counters. Parent validation order in step2 is: validate SUCCESS message/size; admit owned copy; strict parse; exact shape/policy/root; canonical byte equality; finite copy screen; private base64 decode/count/hash integrity. Before ALL these checks complete, publicResponseHashes is NULL on every BUILD failure (including strict parse, shape, root, canonical, screen and decode failure). Never copy candidate/worker-provided hash values into a failure as trusted public facts. Valid independently observed strict evidence may be preserved; otherwise null.

After all step2 checks complete, helper sets lastArtifact to the complete validated artifact and its recomputed public stream hashes, then step3 computes expectedCopyPin. A later step3 pin-construction exception is BUILD with nonnull publicResponseHashes, null expectedCopyPin and no C; this is the explicitly observed-complete-artifact subcase. No callback intervenes before PERSIST/ATTEMPTED. Failure tests cover both BUILD substates; current Design43 after-artifact rule is refined to mean after this complete parent validation, not after an unvalidated worker SUCCESS. REFUSAL, protocol/timeout/OOM, and all step2 rejections always have null public hashes.

At post-persist step5, malformed/invalid UTF8/BOM/duplicate public stdout produces parsed=null and uses the same fixed classifier, yielding a CLASSIFICATION_FAILURE for nonNORMAL output (normally CLI_FAILURE_NO_VERDICT), not a parse exception merely because syntax is malformed. This applies to byte fallback or marker-collision public bytes. Raw duplicate refusal earlier still has no copy and UNSUPPORTED_SCHEMA/REFUSED; it cannot reach step5. A public-stdout parser RESOURCE_PARSE_LIMIT is distinct and raises a PERSIST/CALLBACK_RETURNED EXCEPTION_FAILURE with completed copy/strict/hash metadata. Internal/corruption/decode/projection/classifier exceptions also remain that PERSIST exception; no unbounded fallback. Tests keep complete valid output classification byte/semantic equivalent to accepted24 and verify these separate outcomes.

## Normative58 clarification: closed journal binding, dispatch receipt and transform ABI (55B1/C1)

executePacketProcedure successor has exactly TWO new callbacks (transform, writeJournal) AND one new non-callback parameter journalBinding. This is an explicit signature change beyond helper24. journalBinding has EXACT keys {scopeId,producerIdentity,transformWorkerPin}. producerIdentity is the new closed REQUESTER_PRODUCER variant defined below, not the Design43 core/entry variant. It contains executing requester identity, ordered five-pin source closure and runtime/startup, with core fields explicitly null. transformWorkerPin is the exact path/hash/bytes fifth literal pin. The trusted TEMPLATE derives this binding from the admitted SOURCE/run scope and literal pins, validates runtime/startup/source identity before calling HELPER. HELPER refuses accessors/missing/extra/unsupported fields, takes immutable own-data clones, rechecks scope/identity/ordered closure and worker equality with the supplied producer closure. No packet/model-controlled worker pin or arbitrary context override. ScopeId is not inferred from BASE. Source/AST and primary provenance must prove the trusted caller's derivation; merely accepting an untrusted caller's binding is not authentication.

Change writeDispatch return from undefined to EXACT {pin,ownedBytes}. TEMPLATE owns existing rich dispatch serialization and physically writes it once with the existing synchronous wx/fsync/readback writer, then returns a separate owned-byte copy and exact derived pin. HELPER uses callSync with this new closed-return kind (removes writeDispatch from its VOID callback set), validates returned own-data/types, derives dispatch path from admitted BASE/date, strict-parses/canonical-checks those bytes under the fixed dispatch kind, hashes/counts them, and compares to returned pin. It checks the dispatch record's scope, producerIdentity, actualStartUtc and packet/hash/count facts against journalBinding and the immutable request facts already passed to writeDispatch. Source pins/binding/transport checks required by Design43 remain. Physical persistence is SOURCE-proved by the TEMPLATE writer; byte equality is independently checked by HELPER and later primary provenance. A callback mutation of its byte copy or pin after getters refuses. Only after these checks does dispatchCompleted become true and REQUEST begin. A malformed/mismatched receipt is WRITE_DISPATCH/DISPATCH_BINDING_INVALID, no request/journal; physical D can be absent or a complete matching primary under the existing100010/110010 rows. Physical partial/uncertain/unmatched records keep their earlier precedence. No callback-owned future hash is trusted.

HELPER now has all closed inputs to construct RESPONSE_OBSERVED: scope/identity from journalBinding, verified dispatchPin from the receipt, full BEFORE facts from its private capture, resourcePolicy from CONTEXT, actualUtc from its mechanism. It constructs TRANSFORM_STARTED after O readback with those same values, O pin, transformWorkerPin and registry-derived launch intent. There is ONE serializer owner: HELPER invokes CONTEXT canonical serializer/screen; TEMPLATE writes exact finished bytes only. Provenance independently validates J/S against primary D, binding/source/template and its registry, not returned helper booleans.

writeJournal receives exactly {kind,path,expectedPin,ownedBytes}; kind O/S, path derived from BASE/date, expectedPin computed before attempt, ownedBytes a separate Buffer copy. callSync refuses async/generator/Promise/thenable. It returns exactly {path,sha256,bytes}; own-data/getter/callback-byte hashes/counts must match before COMMITTED. Actual TEMPLATE writer is synchronous wx/fsync/readback. O attempt/readback precedes S attempt/readback; no Worker before both verified pins. Throw/readback failure retains precise attempted state/expected pin, never invented verified pin. Negatives include forged producer/scope/worker/dispatch pin, mismatched journal bytes versus binding/D/BEFORE, wrong callback return shape, async writer and getter/byte corruption.

transform stays asynchronous and receives exactly {kind:'RESPONSE_TRANSFORM_REQUEST',workerPin,resourcePolicyId,senderBefore,stdout,stderr}. SenderBefore is the closed30-key BEFORE projection (error subrecords only {code}); pin/projection are own-data clones; stdout/stderr are exactly-sized dedicated ArrayBuffer copies, lengths equal retained counts. No pools/tails/SAB/accessors/raw string aliases/capture/callbacks. TEMPLATE validates and rehashes immediately before constructor, transfers only the two arrays; Worker repeats checks against CONTEXT. HELPER enters BUILD with known initial evidence BEFORE calling it, but those initial false flags do not describe anything after an untrusted callback failure.

TEMPLATE owns Worker constructor/events/message validation/15s deadline/5s termination grace. It catches its own failures and normally returns exactly {kind:'RESPONSE_TRANSFORM_RESULT',transformEvidence,outcome}; outcome is validated SUCCESS or REFUSAL, and evidence reports only observed events. CONTEXT owns protocol/code/flag predicates; HELPER revalidates. A closed validated result can prove no Worker was constructed or its exit observed. Any rejected Promise, invalid result/message/return getter or lost observation channel which lacks that trusted evidence MUST become TRANSFORM_EXIT_UNCERTAIN and the UNKNOWN branch below; it cannot retain initialized workerCreated:false or use a generic UNRECOGNIZED_CODE to suppress process closure.

Known transformEvidence keeps the existing exact field schema, including real booleans and nullable exitCode. Add a disjoint closed UNKNOWN branch with EXACT keys {observationState:'UNKNOWN_AFTER_CALLBACK_FAILURE',workerPin,policyId,errorCode:'TRANSFORM_EXIT_UNCERTAIN',forceRequesterExit:true,deadlineMs,terminationGraceMs}. It contains NO worker-created/exit/termination event flags, since those facts are unavailable. It is allowed only on BUILD EXCEPTION_FAILURE, full BEFORE, committed O/S, no copy/expected pin/public hashes/result, strict evidence null unless independently completed before loss. It is forbidden in NORMAL/CLASSIFICATION and never counts as success. This branch is a necessary typed schema extension, not coercion of unknowns to false.

After executePacketProcedure synchronously attempted failure storage and RETURNS, actual TEMPLATE checks EVIDENCE: UNKNOWN branch OR KNOWN workerCreated:true with exitObserved:false, independently of chosen errorCode. These conditions force exit; errorCode never suppresses it. It requests termination on its retained Worker handle if any, unrefs it, then invokes process.exit(1), with no later writes/exit hooks. Failure-store uncertainty still attempts exit and makes no complete failure claim. This post-return action is an intent described by UNKNOWN.forceRequesterExit, not falsely recorded as an earlier termination event. Earlier observed known timeout/error cleanup can record terminationRequested:true; if its grace expires it returns KNOWN EXIT_UNCERTAIN with !exitObserved. Executor retains original verified process handle for external closure if Node join blocks, exactly as lifecycle section; no guessed PID/replay. Terminal only runs after requesterClosureAsserted:true and preserves UNKNOWN as an unusable observation. New tests inject rejection after constructor, invalid result, throw during message validation, failure-writer exception and no observed exit.

The generic phase6 BUILD row explicitly excludes it.

For valid public JSON stdout, JSON encoding its extracted result string cannot require more bytes than that string's already escaped JSON token inside the <=12MiB public stdout; therefore the result field plus bounded fixed fields fits the16MiB trust profile. For malformed stdout parsed=null, no raw string becomes result. ## Normative58 required decoded screen and complete failure rows (55C2-C4)

Add DISPATCH_BINDING_INVALID,PUBLIC_COPY_INVALID,PUBLIC_COPY_PIN_INVALID to the closed EXCEPTION errorCode union (not sender or worker enums). In parent BUILD step2, CONTEXT validatePublicResponseArtifact performs canonical-copy byte screen and the MODE-SPECIFIC decoded stdout/stderr screen defined below, requiring zero non-allowlisted hits. It must not byte-screen JSON text in place of its decoded keys/leaves. It owns this application predicate, not only matcher definitions. Strict owned-copy/base64/count/hash checks precede these stream screens. Failure is PUBLIC_COPY_SCREEN_REFUSED/BUILD, hashes null/no C. Provenance's SAME pure artifact validator independently decodes the primary copy and applies these same stream screens; otherwise a helper-only omission could escape the base64 screen. Finite ASCII/escaped/Unicode policy limits remain explicit; no universal privacy claim or reconstruction of original bytes. Successful in-budget output containing the permitted exact fictional literal is preserved by the same allowlist.

ONE normative transform-code predicate replaces ALL inherited correlation/order rules. KNOWN exact17-field evidence carries parentErrorCode selected from observed errors OOM > PROTOCOL_REFUSED > WORKER_ERROR > null, and workerOutcomeCode from the valid REFUSAL or null. UNKNOWN remains the exact7-field union. At completed callback/cleanup, derive final transformEvidence.errorCode in this precedence: UNKNOWN or created&&!exitObserved -> EXIT_UNCERTAIN; else deadlineExpired -> TIMEOUT; else parentErrorCode OOM -> OOM; else parentErrorCode PROTOCOL_REFUSED OR WORKER_ERROR -> PROTOCOL; else exitObserved&&exitCode!=0 -> EXIT; else valid worker REFUSAL -> workerOutcomeCode; else never-created -> STARTUP; else fullvalidSUCCESS/ack/cleanexit -> null; any remaining invalid completion -> PROTOCOL with normalized parentErrorCode PROTOCOL_REFUSED. Runtime/source code uses the same pure derivation as provenance and terminal; a known disagreement is CONTENT_MISMATCH. Lower-priority observations are retained in fields, never another admissible primary code.

| Final code | Required correlation after higher-priority conditions excluded |
|---|---|
| TRANSFORM_EXIT_UNCERTAIN | UNKNOWN forceRequesterExit branch OR KNOWN workerCreated && !exitObserved && terminationRequested after cleanup; regardless other deadline/error/refusal |
| TRANSFORM_TIMEOUT | deadlineExpired and (not workerCreated OR terminationRequested), observed exit if created; parent errors/refusal may coexist but are lower |
| TRANSFORM_OOM | no deadline/unknown; parentErrorCode ERR_WORKER_OUT_OF_MEMORY, actual exit observed; refusal may coexist |
| TRANSFORM_PROTOCOL | no deadline/unknown/OOM; parentErrorCode PROTOCOL_REFUSED OR WORKER_ERROR, actual exit if created; this INCLUDES uncaught worker/stack-overflow crash with exit1 |
| TRANSFORM_EXIT | no higher error; actual observed nonzero exit, parentErrorCode:null; workerOutcomeCode may coexist |
| worker-local REFUSAL code | no higher condition; resultMessageObserved:true and exact workerOutcomeCode; source ack required except self-check TRANSFORM_STARTUP; exit observed0 if created |
| TRANSFORM_STARTUP before construction | workerConstructionAttempted:false for prehash refusal, or true for constructor throw; created/online/source/result/exit false, exitCode:null,parentErrorCode:null,workerOutcomeCode:null |
| null | full source/validSUCCESS/result/observed0exit; no deadline/termination/error; both parentErrorCode/workerOutcomeCode null |

Deadline flag may be observed timer OR measured monotonic elapsed expiry during startup; never-created TIMEOUT is allowed without a termination attempt on a nonexistent Worker. It never implies universal interruption of synchronous work. For created Worker, no observed exit after grace always selects EXIT_UNCERTAIN first. Actual TEMPLATE forces exit on UNKNOWN or created&&!exitObserved irrespective code; missing trustworthy settling still uses external original verified handle only, no invented F. REFUSAL plus deadline selects TIMEOUT when exit known; refusal+OOM selects OOM; unobserved exit selects EXIT_UNCERTAIN. Tests include uncaught worker crash/RangeError WORKER_ERROR+exit1 -> PROTOCOL/110010NONE/UNUSABLE; crash+deadline -> TIMEOUT/NONE/UNUSABLE when exited, else EXIT_UNCERTAIN/NONE/UNUSABLE only after actual requester closure; forged correlations still CONTENT_MISMATCH. Worker-local subset remains exact9codes; no parent-event code self-report.

Explicit parent failure enumeration, no normal result/copy write for BUILD failures:

| Failure | exception stage/state/errorCode | public hashes / expected pin / primary closing |
|---|---|---|
| SUCCESS copy strict parse exceeds fixed profile | BUILD/BUILDING_IN_MEMORY/RESOURCE_PARSE_LIMIT | null/null/C absent;110010 NONE/UNUSABLE after all earlier phases |

Generic BUILD closing row explicitly includes PUBLIC_COPY_INVALID/PUBLIC_COPY_PIN_INVALID and parent screen/parse rejections, retaining RAW_STDOUT_DUPLICATE exclusion/phase4 precedence. Both would-be normal and classification records become EXCEPTION_FAILURE with result:null,responsePin:null and no MD, full immutable BEFORE evidence and the known C expected pin/strict/hashes/J/S/worker facts retained from procedure return. ## Closed REQUESTER producer variant and explicit D/R/F delta (56B1)

REQUESTER_PRODUCER has EXACT keys: producerType:'REQUESTER',executingRequesterPin,closurePins,workerDependencyPins,corePath,coreSha256,coreExecuted,runtimeRoot,nodeVersion,nodeExecPath,nodeBinarySha256,execArgv,nodeOptionsPresent. executingRequesterPin is the executing adopted requester path/hash/bytes, checked against admitted template derivation and actual argv/module/startup. closurePins is EXACT ordered [READER,RENDER_POLICY,HELPER,REQUESTER_VERIFIER,TRANSFORM_WORKER] (five pins, does not include main). workerDependencyPins is EXACT ordered [READER,RENDER_POLICY,HELPER], equal to those three entries and the worker's admitted literal subclosure. Main executes/imports first four after all-five prechecks; the fifth is a checked worker source subject, not a main import. corePath:null,coreSha256:null,coreExecuted:false are mandatory; they explicitly assert no core/entry/config executes here. No selected-A1 reference masquerades as executing producer. A selected core stays only in its separate existing admission/selection reference.

Runtime/startup fields mean the existing checked canonical PC root, exact Node/version/binary pin, execArgv:[],nodeOptionsPresent:false. Closure values are exact pins, not URLs inferred by packet fields. The TEMPLATE derives/clones this variant from SOURCE/admission/literal main and worker identities; HELPER validates its closed shape/equalities, not an arbitrary assertion of executed bytes. Hash/import/self-attestation limitations remain. ## F1: option-A context-free escape-decoded byte screening (B1/N1)

Keep the existing whole-byte finite screen with escapeAware:true. Add the SAME policy's linear context-free escape-decoded view for BOTH public BYTE_FALLBACK streams, including stderr alongside normal JSON stdout. This does not change ORIGINAL byte redaction: no mapped encoded masks, original spans, markers or original budget semantics change. Screen the added view with escapeAware:false and the exact existing allowlist. Any non-allowlisted hit, including screen-counter saturation, yields PUBLIC_COPY_SCREEN_REFUSED for the whole copy. No C/hash/pin/result promotion and no repeat.

The view is generated by one forward cursor, from original public bytes only, independent of quotes, token validity, envelope validity and raw newlines. Recognized simple escapes are quote, backslash, slash, b, f, n, r, t. For a maximal backslash run of length n, emit floor(n/2) literal backslashes; if n is odd, the remaining backslash may introduce one recognized simple escape or u followed by exactly four ASCII hex digits. Decode that unit to UTF8. Adjacent escaped high/low surrogate units combine to their scalar; an unpaired escaped surrogate becomes U+FFFD as Buffer.from of the corresponding JS string does. For an odd unknown/malformed escape or a dangling backslash, retain the remaining backslash and unconsumed bytes literally. Never recursively decode emitted bytes. Even backslash runs leave the following u/escape letter literal. All other original bytes, including invalid raw UTF8 in a labelled malformed byte stream, pass through; no lenient envelope parse or Unicode-normalization claim.

The output byte count is <= input byte count: literal bytes/pairs do not expand, every simple escape shrinks, a six-byte Unicode escape emits <=three bytes, and a twelve-byte pair <=four. Allocate at most that already approved stream cap, with a checked cursor. Release each view before making the next. No array of per-token values, per-byte origin maps, quote pairing, repeated suffix scanning or per-token regex overhead; total view construction/scanning is linear under the accepted finite cursors. Raw and decoded screening budgets are public-only and distinct from ORIGINAL transformation charges. Retaining the raw screen is intentional: it keeps its existing escapeAware behavior, while the extra view can only add refusals. A hit already refuses, so duplicate detection across the two views cannot create a successful artifact or expand the original budget.

The public-only validator below owns this entire predicate and is called by the worker producer, runtime parent and public provenance. Worker-generated invalid artifact failures other than the fixed nine worker refusal codes remain protocol/mechanism defects, never a wider worker enum. Option-A decoded hits use the existing allowed worker PUBLIC_COPY_SCREEN_REFUSED code. The stronger screen is finite assurance only; arbitrary encodings, malformed unknown escapes, confusables and privacy absence remain unproved.

Stdout mode remains PASS iff JSON_ENVELOPE; MALFORMED_OR_INCOMPLETE iff BYTE_FALLBACK; withholding iff NOT_ATTEMPTED_WITHHELD. Parent BYTE stdout still strict-parses: valid unique JSON is invalid as byte mode; JSON_SYNTAX/JSON_DUPLICATE can remain labelled malformed and never usable stdout. This allows a genuine later-syntax-error original where byte masks collide into duplicate marker keys. Independently detected ORIGINAL duplicate still refuses RAW_STDOUT_DUPLICATE before any copy. Refuse BYTE_FALLBACK_AFTER_JSON_ERROR in BOTH streams, with no fallback override. Withheld empty bytes keep their exact count/reason predicates.

F1 falsifiers: normal JSON stdout plus encoded sensitive stderr (new whole-copy loss), malformed stdout diagnostic loss, both stream positions, malformed prefix/trailing garbage, stray odd quote and raw-control/newline quote-parity defeat, valid later escape after invalid candidate, simple escapes/even and odd slash runs/dangling/invalid Unicode, escaped surrogate pair and lone surrogate, raw invalid UTF8, allowed fictional literal, saturation normalization. More than100000 quote candidates within byte cap must NOT refuse solely for count. Construct finite sensitive fixtures from character codes; persist no raw fixture payload or literal private address. List all frozen62-accepted corpus copies newly refused by this added predicate.

## F2: original-derived exact transformation before persistence

Current public shape/hash/finite screen checks cannot independently establish span fidelity. The parent already owns original capture buffers. Add one fixed pure verification entry which receives that private owned capture, admitted parser identity and candidate owned canonical-copy bytes. It independently derives the expected transformation from the original under the policy rather than trusting worker rows/status/claimed hashes.

Suggested fixed pure entries (implementation naming may differ only with the call-site census updated): producePublicResponseCopy(capture,parserPin) for the worker mechanism and validatePublicResponseAgainstOriginal(copy,capture,parserPin) for the parent. Both use one internal authoritative redaction planner/render algorithm in CONTEXT. The latter recomputes from private originals; it does not call/import the worker/helper transform, accept its plan or pass caller-configured limits/render callbacks. Shared policy code is intentional; independence means separately computed private-original facts in the parent, not a second conflicting policy implementation.

Derivation order:

- Derive incomplete reasons/withholding from primitive private capture exactly as62 (truncation/timedOut/childError/missingClose/overflow/signal). Withheld streams have empty bytes/no rows/null original hash; derive the exact status and all root/stream scalar fields. Sender stdin/kill facts keep their accepted completion semantics.
- For complete stdout rerun fixed raw strict parsing on the private original. PASS, MALFORMED/UTF8/BOM, DUPLICATE and resource refusal are independent observations. Raw duplicate or resource refusal cannot be disguised as successful byte fallback. Match observed worker strict evidence only after that derivation. Genuine withholding never parses incomplete raw prefixes.
- Original transformation budgets and marker sequence begin fresh and are cumulative stdout then stderr. The same finite scanner counts every non-allowlisted hit BEFORE merge, merges overlaps with exact pattern-ID order, checks charge before row append and derives ordinal paths only when a hit exists.
- BYTE_FALLBACK: derive spans from ORIGINAL retained bytes with escapeAware:true. Expected replacement is exact concatenation of untouched source ranges and derived markers. Validate sorted nonoverlapping in-range coordinates through derived equality, not trusting declared endpoints. Expected rows/markers must equal the declared whole row sequence, including pattern-ID order; bounds/hash/length checks alone are insufficient.
- JSON_ENVELOPE: derive rows in original parsed Object.keys/array traversal order. Paths are ordinal original entries/values, never raw sensitive key names. Derive KEY marker selection using original-key reservation and already emitted-key collision avoidance; all spans in one redacted key legitimately share one marker. Global counter gaps caused by collisions are deterministic. VALUE markers use the same sequence; derive normalization and row coordinates from decoded UTF8. Numeric parsing/key-order/lone-surrogate behavior remains accepted24/62, not an arbitrary new verbatim guarantee.
- If no JSON stdout row exists, expected public stdout is EXACT original bytes. If any exists, expected stdout is the accepted compact canonical serialization of the deterministically transformed parsed value, with the same number/Unicode/key rules. Untouched strings retain parsed JSON representation; a redacted string undergoes the existing Buffer-based normalization of lone surrogates. Stderr always follows the fixed byte path when complete.
- Independently derive all stream/root fields, metadata charge, observed/retained counts, status, coordinate, normalization, public hashes and absence/nullness of original hashes. No original/span hashes or raw sensitive values appear in errors, journals or new public fields. Existing warnings about normalized JSON and finite privacy remain accurate; add that runtime parent checks exact original-derived policy transformation.

Memory implementation: use a bounded internal plan and streaming compare renderer, not a second complete public-copy buffer or a second transformed object graph. Strict raw graph is bounded by the existing profile. It, its plan and closures holding original values are released at the block boundary BEFORE the shared public-only validator parses decoded public stdout leaves. Exact sequencing is specified below; releasing reachability is not an immediate GC/RSS guarantee. Plan holds at most4096 charged metadata rows (~1MiB), no retained raw key/value strings beyond the parsed original graph. A single traversal stack of depth<=128 and push/pop ordinal components replaces per-node copied chain arrays. Construct entryPath only for actual rows, with2048 cap; children do not allocate path strings just because they exist.

The internal renderer has two fixed modes owned by policy: worker page collector and parent comparator. These are internal mechanism choices, not public caller callbacks/options. Comparator walks existing decoded candidate stream bytes with a byte cursor and reports fixed PUBLIC_COPY_INVALID at the first difference, including missing/extra tails. It checks the full expected metadata sequence too. Expose no generic caller-chosen sink. Worker collector retains existing finite byte/page caps. Same JSON quoting implementation emits <=16Ki UTF16 runs without splitting surrogate pairs. The exact incremental transformed-string decoder is TextDecoder(utf8,{fatal:true,ignoreBOM:true}), with streaming calls and final flush, preserving leading/internal U+FEFF and partial code points. Transformed JS-string bytes are valid UTF8 because Buffer.from normalizes lone surrogates and ASCII splice boundaries cannot cut code points; unchanged strings retain parsed JSON semantics. If an implementation cannot maintain this equivalence it must refuse design/source readiness, not silently weaken compare to a hash or test label.

Avoid a second canonical-copy buffer in parent shape validation as well: strict-parse candidate, then use a fixed internally bounded canonical-byte comparator against the existing owned copy. Preserve COPY depth/node/byte controls and exact JSON2+LF. No JSON.stringify of an unbounded graph and no reconstructed full base64/copy buffer merely for comparison. Decoded candidate stdout/stderr buffers remain bounded and live through AFTER classification as needed.

The parent pure entry returns one CLOSED internal two-arm data union, not an unstructured exception carrying raw content:

- success exact keys kind,artifact,decoded,publicResponseHashes,strictParseEvidence, with kind VALIDATED_ARTIFACT and independent original-derived strict evidence matching the complete artifact;
- refusal exact keys kind,errorCode,strictParseEvidence,publicResponseHashes, with kind VALIDATION_REFUSAL, exactly one of RAW_STDOUT_DUPLICATE, PUBLIC_COPY_INVALID, PUBLIC_COPY_SCREEN_REFUSED, UNRECOGNIZED_CODE and publicResponseHashes:null. These four are the closed parent-validation refusal enum, NOT the worker-nine enum or the entire exception union. No artifact/decoded/original field. strictParseEvidence is the independently observed COMPLETE original decision or null when none completed.

The parent derives private original completeness/raw strict decision FIRST within this pure entry, before admitting copy fields as safe. Original DUPLICATE returns RAW_STDOUT_DUPLICATE with independent DUPLICATE_REFUSED evidence. Original RESOURCE_PARSE_LIMIT after clean worker SUCCESS maps PUBLIC_COPY_INVALID and null strict decision, because it is an original-side disagreement with that success. This prevents preserving a forged worker MALFORMED/PASS root on an independently detected raw duplicate. Known passed/malformed/withheld strict evidence remains available on a later canonical/screen/fidelity refusal. Mechanism consumes this closed union, sets its last independently observed strict evidence, then throws only the fixed normalized code into its existing BUILD failure path. Do not lose or invent root evidence via generic catch, compare it only to the correct private source, or reinterpret the parent-refusal DTO as the worker's narrower9-code union. Unexpected observation loss retains UNKNOWN/TRANSFORM_EXIT_UNCERTAIN rules. Internal pure catches normalize fixed codes with no raw strings; true process death has no fabricated returned union.

Errors before completion of ALL parent validation have publicResponseHashes:null, expectedCopyPin:null, senderBEFORE and zero response writes. Never promote worker/candidate hashes to trusted safe facts. Invalid rows/path/order/marker/fidelity/root/private status are PUBLIC_COPY_INVALID. Declared metadata/count/path excess is invalid artifact and maps PUBLIC_COPY_INVALID (F5), rather than leaking RESOURCE_METADATA_BYTES from a corrupt candidate. Every original-side budget/path/parse/public-copy disagreement after clean worker SUCCESS maps PUBLIC_COPY_INVALID, not a genuine worker RESOURCE_* refusal. Duplicate is the sole original-side exception and preserves phase4 UNSUPPORTED_SCHEMA. Genuine worker RESOURCE_* refusals retain their existing finite codes and do not reach recomputation. Public screen failures stay PUBLIC_COPY_SCREEN_REFUSED. Only after all original-derived, canonical, shape/ABI and screen checks succeed may safe hashes and expected copy pin be derived and PERSIST begin. Record/callback mutation guards and step5 strict classifier remain unchanged.

Public provenance boundary: private-original fidelity is checked at runtime while the parent holds originals; they are NOT persisted. Later pure verifyReviewProvenance cannot independently recreate that private relation from public journals/copy hashes or a boolean. It must retain admitted source/closure/protocol and public correlation checks and explicitly disclose this limit. Do not add a self-attested fidelityVerified flag as replacement proof or claim cryptographic/verbatim raw-source fidelity. F2 prevents a defective worker from altering a parent's accepted result within this source-admitted runtime boundary; execution/hash/import/forgery limits remain.

F2 falsifiers: change untouched verdict text while some unrelated span exists; dropped/reordered/repeated/moved rows; altered offset/pattern-ID/order/marker/path/kind; extra/missing replacement, misleading literal marker text, multiple KEY spans sharing a marker, reserved-key marker skips, integer-key order, arrays/empty containers/mixed Unicode and lone surrogates; zero-span exact bytes versus redacted canonical normalization; forged raw status/withheld reasons/original counts; forged duplicate raw stdout; same payload hashes with changed policy metadata; every failure before C/hash/pin promotion. Compare collector output to comparator as an internal-consistency check only, mutate each independent fact, AND run the frozen62 differential specified below to detect common-mode planner/renderer errors. Same-planner agreement alone cannot accept F2.

## F3: timer uses actual monotonic observation

Keep one exact monotonic start before worker rehash/construction and raw elapsed observation per handler. Deadline callback now samples performance.now once and runs observeDeadline. If elapsed<15000, re-arm for Math.max(1,Math.ceil(15000-elapsed)) and make no termination/deadline claim. If expired, set deadlineExpired through the same observation predicate and stop at that same timestamp. This changes no numeric value. Event-loop delays remain unbounded; it is not hard wall-time enforcement.

EVERY finish(now), including grace and never-created finish, calls observeDeadline(now) before evidence derivation. Natural observed late exit keeps exitObserved:true/terminationRequested:false unless a terminate call actually occurred. Grace is created only after a real termination request, once. Settled guards/timer cleanup/deep-frozen evidence stay. Display elapsed is ceil; !deadlineExpired implies displayed elapsed<=deadline, allowing14999.5→15000. Any optional extra correlation must preserve that case. Test fake early timer14999.x/re-arm with no termination, exact15000 expiry, delayed exit before timer, original late-message/no-exit cases and post-settlement timer creation absence. The deadline timer stays active after a protocol fault: protocol at14990 then observed exit after15000 selects TIMEOUT over protocol, retaining the protocol observation. Grace crossing deadline also observes expiry; if no exit, EXIT_UNCERTAIN still has higher precedence. Test both cases. The exact instrumentation has THREE setTimeout sites (initial, early re-arm, grace), not the frozen62 two; assert all substitution counts before publication. Synthetic cases do not prove OS scheduler behavior.

## F4: IPC-owned outcome without redundant clones, bounded traversal (N4/N5)

Remove BOTH large outcome clones. Real Worker IPC has already copied non-transferred data; the transferred ArrayBuffer is exclusively parent-owned and detached in the sender. The supervisor retains that deserialized message, validates it and returns the same owned carrier at finish. The sender cannot mutate that owned outcome later. FakeWorker must emit a freshly copied IPC-shaped message, detaching/moving buffers where transfer is modelled, instead of keeping an alias to the parent's object. Mutation of the fake sender's original source must not change receipt; mutation through an intentionally shared fake alias is an invalid fixture. Small evidence gets an explicit fresh finite copy with worker pin copied and deep freeze; ArrayBuffers are not claimed frozen.

ACK validation checks exact own data fields, closed pins and exactly the expected finite dependency-array length before serialization/equality. SUCCESS remains exact five-key bounded carrier, copy ArrayBuffer<=24MiB; REFUSAL exact four keys/nine codes. Reject accessors, extra fields and SharedArrayBuffer. Validation errors inside message handling use existing fixed protocol observation and cleanup. IPC deserialization ALREADY occurred before handler validation: these limits only bound post-receipt work and cannot prevent a giant malicious message's allocation. Worker old-generation/stack resourceLimits do NOT bound ArrayBuffer or Buffer backing stores/serializer pages; they are not a whole-worker or whole-process RAM cap.

Finish performs no large allocation/clone; it observes time, derives precedence, snapshots small evidence, clears timers and resolves once. Late events make no changes or new timer. Global/parent OOM can still prevent any record. Preserve UNKNOWN-after-transform callback loss and original-handle cleanup. The local fictional bench never exits Codex or the owner's apps; actual forced requester exit remains a later SOURCE requirement.

Planner/renderer use one push/pop ordinal stack with bounded live depth<=128; build path only for charged hits. No per-node copied ancestor chain or second transformed graph. Fixed internal worker collector/parent comparator own their sinks; no caller callback/options. All original/stream/page/row/graph limits still apply. Memory use is finite but not guaranteed <=256MiB or below a previous sample.

NEW wide/deep fixture DATA preflight already passed:99865 siblings at depth128,100000values+keys,8MiB+1MiB,260+3836hits=4096,metadata1047995bytes. It was outside actual61 and is feasibility only. Use it for one new successor Worker profile, plus a no-hit wide/deep variant if needed to isolate path allocation. Sample parent BUILD step2 separately from worker transformation, report new worker/parent wall and whole-process RSS samples. Compare honestly to the previous392622080-byte (about374.4MiB) sampled process RSS, which was a DIFFERENT layout and not peak: no causal reduction claim or same-layout speedup. No old profile/OOM/native/requester launcher replay.

The new private recomputation/public-only validation is reached only after a fully validated clean worker SUCCESS. Its wall time and allocations are OUTSIDE15s worker watchdog and256MiB worker heap; parent has NO hard deadline. Parent work can roughly double and structural validation may add further cost; actual timing must be measured, never assumed. Block-scoped graph-release sequencing below prevents concurrent strong reachability of original RAW graph and public stdout leaf graph, not immediate reclamation. In-budget differential corpus uses fresh pure calls to frozen62 functions as a reference, never the consumed old profile/requester launchers.

## N2/N5: one public-only predicate, runtime-only private fidelity and sequencing

Public entry validatePublicResponseArtifact(copy,parserPin) takes ONLY owned public copy bytes and fixed admitted parser pin. It strict-validates fixed COPY framing/canonical bytes/shape/count/base64/hash/ABI, then mode-specific screens: strict public JSON stdout decoded keys/leaves with escapeAware:false, BYTE streams with the old raw screen AND F1 context-free view, withheld streams with exact empty invariants. It returns the existing fixed artifact/decoded/publicResponseHashes facts on success; public structure/limit/syntax errors map PUBLIC_COPY_INVALID, finite hits/saturation PUBLIC_COPY_SCREEN_REFUSED, unexpected caught errors UNRECOGNIZED_CODE. No RAW_STDOUT_DUPLICATE from public bytes; malformed public byte duplicate is not original duplicate. Shared code owns all predicates, not only regex definitions.

Runtime validatePublicResponseAgainstOriginal(copy,capture,parserPin) uses this exact public entry PLUS private fidelity, in this order:
1. Only after clean validated worker SUCCESS, enter a private-validation block. Derive independent raw strict decision and original withholding FIRST. Duplicate returns the explicit refusal immediately; no completed raw decision yields null, never worker-claimed root.
2. Within that block, use a fixed internal structural decoder for the candidate canonical copy (no public stdout-leaf graph yet), derive ORIGINAL plan and compare every expected byte/row/root/stream scalar. No safe hash/pin promotion. The original graph can coexist with canonical-copy metadata/base64 graph and decoded stream buffers; it must not coexist with a public stdout leaf graph. Invalid original/structure/fidelity returns closed parent refusal as below.
3. Leave that block, release references to original parsed graph/plan, candidate structural graph/decoded buffers and any closure holding raw parsed values. Keep only completed small independent strict evidence and original capture byte buffers. No GC/RSS guarantee. No second complete canonical copy or transformed graph is built.
4. Invoke EXACT validatePublicResponseArtifact(copy,parserPin), repeating bounded structural parsing/decoding to share the full public predicate with provenance. This repetition is intentional cost, preferable here to a caller-supplied bypass/prevalidation token. It now may create the public stdout leaf graph after original graph reachability is gone. Compare returned root to independent small evidence, then return VALIDATED_ARTIFACT. On its refusal retain independent strict evidence and null hashes.

The worker producer releases its original plan/parsed graph before it invokes the same public entry on the generated copy. A public finite refusal is the existing worker-nine screen code. A generated shape/limit/unknown defect not in worker-nine is a mechanism failure observed as protocol, not a new worker refusal. The worker has no public-fidelity attestation field.

Pure later verifyReviewProvenance calls EXACT validatePublicResponseArtifact on primary public bytes, with no private capture, optional screen callback or alternate token rule. Test runtime/public-only/provenance agreement on every PUBLIC predicate, including byte escaped hits and saturation. Private-original fidelity cannot be re-established from public artifacts; source-admitted runtime checking, protocol and public correlation remain its boundary, with hash/import/execution/forgery limits unchanged. No self-attested flag or original/span hash is added.

Parent success exact keys:kind,artifact,decoded,publicResponseHashes,strictParseEvidence; kind VALIDATED_ARTIFACT. Sender allowance and worker-nine remain unchanged. safeOS exceptions and PUBLIC_COPY_PIN_INVALID are later mechanism failures, not members of this pure validation union.

Every parent-union refusal row below has failedStep BUILD_RESPONSE_COPY, responsePersistenceState BUILDING_IN_MEMORY, sender BEFORE, responseJournalState START_COMMITTED with both exact expected/verified journal pins, fully KNOWN clean worker SUCCESS facts (construction/created/online/source/result/exit true, exit0,errorCode:null,parentErrorCode:null,workerOutcomeCode:null,deadline:false,termination:false). Explicitly SUPERSEDES contract58 statement that a duplicate ALWAYS returns worker REFUSAL, its worker-only root/error correlation and missing clean-SUCCESS/parent-duplicate row. Only actual O/S physical attempts can use that uncertainty code, with their existing attempted states/pin resolution. ## Verdict: Review OK
## Scope: GSS FOUNDATION INTAKE60

**What this covers.** This approval has two parts, both within the fictional-only bench and same-review boundary:

1. **Corrective62 CODE and composition.** This covers the files below as rendered whole:
   - policy62 `6D077709…`, transform62 `20506A65…`, supervisor62 `E59FEDBE…`, bench62 `A20C2161…` and worker62 `5C574FF9…`;
   - their publishers;
   - the correction62, events62, bench62 and extra-profile callers and reports.

   I assessed them against all of actual59 (B1, B2, N1–N6, T1), contract58, the Design43 EXCERPTS and helper24.
2. **The transport19 bootstrap.** This covers requester60 `665A9F78…` against frozen17 `F6D95E5C…`, the six substitutions, the checks, builder and verifier changes, the owner approval, the drift diagnosis and the CLI compatibility EXCERPTS.

The V5 natural-late-exit predicate amendment is accepted here on its own merits. I do not assume actual58 covered it.

**Method.** I read the embedded bodies only. I executed nothing and recomputed no hashes. U+0040 is written `\u0040`.

**Context.** The context is sufficient for this boundary:
- All current code and current test callers are whole.
- The identity-only historical bodies are either replaced by whole current equivalents or are evidence-only prefixes or derivations.
- I independently compared the 14 numeric controls in policy62 against the literal V4 value in contract58, and they match.

### Disposition of actual59 findings

**B1. Resolved.**
- The CONTEXT validator now binds PASS⇔JSON_ENVELOPE, MALFORMED⇔BYTE_FALLBACK and WITHHELD⇔withheld for stdout. It refuses stdout BYTE_FALLBACK_AFTER_JSON_ERROR.
- It strict-parses byte-mode stdout and refuses if the parse succeeds.
- A resource-limit hit refuses rather than falling back.

By reading, a genuine fallback cannot become valid JSON after redaction:
- Pattern spans never contain `"` or `\`.
- Markers outside strings begin with `<`.
- Errors inside strings (control characters, bad escapes, invalid UTF-8) lie outside every pattern class.
- Escape adjustment only ever leaves a valid escape or an invalid `\<`.
- A genuine fallback cannot hit RESOURCE_PARSE_LIMIT at the parent either, because the raw pass already ran under the same node and depth limits and redaction does not add nodes.

In bench step 5, byte-mode strict parsing therefore yields `parsed=null`, so `record.result` is null. The forged-escaped-`\u0040` negatives and the end-to-end no-copy/no-result case discriminate the actual59 bypass.

**B2. Resolved.**
- Each handler takes its own `performance.now()`, and `deadlineExpired` uses raw elapsed ≥ 15000. Ceil is used for display only.
- `terminationRequested` is set only inside `requestTermination`, which performs a real `terminate()` call.
- `finish` resolves a deep-frozen clone. Every handler, as well as `stop` and `protocol`, returns early once settled, and `finish` clears both timers.
- The V5 predicate drops the `created&&deadlineExpired⇒terminationRequested` requirement but keeps `created&&!exitObserved⇒terminationRequested`. This is the correct truthful rule.
- The precedence in `deriveTransformCode` is unchanged.
- NATURAL_LATE_TIMER_DELAY, FRACTIONAL_EARLY_EXIT and the NO_EXIT late-event cases discriminate.

**N1. Resolved.** The capture now accepts exactly the 28 required fields plus the four known helper24 extras. It checks data descriptors and captures only the required fields. A fake-spawn run through the real `requestPacket` produces 32 keys, 28 private fields and 30 projected fields.

**N2. Resolved.**
- An unexpected error after the source acknowledgement now escapes as a worker crash, which the parent records as WORKER_ERROR and maps to PROTOCOL.
- Screen saturation maps to PUBLIC_COPY_SCREEN_REFUSED.
- A journal construction error is distinguished from a physical attempt.
- `validateTransformResult` correlates message presence, outcome code, strict evidence and `workerOutcomeCode`.
- KNOWN initial evidence is set at BUILD entry, and UNKNOWN replaces it after a callback loss.

**N3 and N4. Accepted as explicit limits**, not fixes. N4 (span fidelity) is carried forward below.

**N5. Resolved.** The real-Worker DUPLICATE and HITS_OVER paths are back, and the transform62 and worker62 derivation checks are present.

**N6. Resolved as finite evidence.**
- Three new layouts saturate 4096 hits, about 1 MiB of metadata charge, 8 MiB plus 1 MiB of input and 100000 nodes (including depth 128).
- Workers took 433–966 ms.
- Whole-process RSS is reported honestly as sampled.

**T1. Resolved.** The valid-JSON mutation returns the expected pin and reaches the hash gate in `verifyPin` (one callback return, zero transforms, journal state ATTEMPTED).

### Transport19 disposition: accepted as a source delta, with limits

- **Exact delta.** Requester60 equals frozen17 with exactly six substitutions. The byte arithmetic reconciles: only the error label grows, by 6 bytes, giving 9688.
- **Unchanged controls.** Args, env stripping, Node and root checks, dispatch-before-spawn, no-replay, capture and the usability predicate are all unchanged.
- **Checks.** `deriveBootstrapRequester` and `assertBootstrapSource` check single occurrences and reverse-equivalence.
- **Help text.** The selected 2.1.299 help blocks are consistent with the intended controls:
  - `--tools ""` disables all tools.
  - `--safe-mode` disables hooks, MCP and CLAUDE.md.
  - `--restricted` ignores user, project and local settings.
  - `--no-session-persistence` and `--output-format` apply under `-p`.
  - Variadic options are each followed by another flag, so none can swallow input.
- **Failure behaviour.** Model fallback, extra model usage or JSON shape drift fail closed through the existing `resolvedModels` and parse predicates.
- **Selection sufficiency.** The selection is adequate for the flags actually passed.

**Limits:**
- Help text is not enforcement proof, and the binary's behaviour change is unreviewed.
- The help states that "managed settings still apply" and that "invalid settings are silently ignored" in `-p` mode. Hooks-off therefore relies on `--safe-mode` plus the assumed absence of managed policy hooks. This residual is inherited from 2.1.283 but is now explicit.
- `--disallowedTools *` may be a no-op literal. It is redundant with `--tools ""`.
- The pinned binary sits inside a VS Code extension directory that auto-updates. That update path already caused this drift, and it will fail closed again, as an ENOENT or hash mismatch.

### Non-blocking findings (carry forward; required before STANDARD unless noted)

**F1. The byte-mode decoded screen is still lexical-only.**
- In byte modes the parent accepts JSON_DUPLICATE. This is necessary, because a genuine marker collision can produce it. For example, a pre-existing literal `<REDACTED:0>` key followed by a redacted key, with a later syntax error, parses to a duplicate at the parent.
- So a defective worker can label raw duplicate-key JSON as MALFORMED, which a genuine worker must refuse. The same applies to near-valid JSON with trailing garbage. Escaped `\u0040` strings then persist in a byte copy that a lenient decoder would decode.
- Strict consumers get no result, and this stays within the disclosed byte-fallback limit for escaped tokens.
- Recommendation: in byte modes, also decode and screen every lexically complete JSON string token with `escapeAware:false`.
- Test gap: add a genuine marker-collision fixture that proves JSON_DUPLICATE is tolerated.

**F2. N4 fidelity.**
- The parent holds the original capture, so byte-mode reconstruction (original with spans replaced by markers, compared to the public bytes) is cheap and exact.
- JSON_ENVELOPE could be checked the same way by reapplying the rows to the strictly re-parsed original strings.
- Without this, a defective worker can still alter verdict text when spans exist. That matters for STANDARD, whose product is the verdict.

**F3. Deadline timer path.**
- The timer callback sets `deadlineExpired` by fiat. Node's millisecond loop clock can fire it up to about 1 ms before `performance` elapsed reaches 15000.
- This is harmless because it makes the cutoff stricter, but the claim "exact elapsed≥15000" holds only for event observations.
- Either `observeDeadline` in the callback and re-arm for the remainder, or state that the timer firing is authoritative.
- Optional hardening: have the validator require `!deadlineExpired ⇒ observedElapsedMs ≤ deadlineMs`.

**F4. Parent memory.**
- The copy ArrayBuffer is cloned twice: `structuredClone(m)` in the message handler, then again in `finish`. That is up to about 72 MiB of extra parent memory on top of the validation buffers.
- An allocation `RangeError` inside `finish` would be an uncaught error in an event handler.
- Sampled RSS of about 352–392 MB already exceeds the 256 MiB worker heap. The owner's final numeric choice must weigh whole-requester memory.
- Recommendation: avoid the double clone.
- Also not profiled: a wide layout at maximum depth. `jsonRedact` allocates a fresh chain array per node, which is O(nodes×depth) transient.

**F5. Closed-profile nits.**
- Stderr BYTE_FALLBACK_AFTER_JSON_ERROR is still admitted. It should be refused symmetrically.
- The `sensitiveSpans` call on journal bytes does not normalize saturation (unreachable with fixed fields).
- Parent metadata overflow yields RESOURCE_METADATA_BYTES, whereas the design's BUILD rows list PUBLIC_COPY_INVALID.
- The inherited `fictional59` and `BENCH59` labels remain in bench62.

**F6. Documentary.**
- `verifyPrimary` (via new59) reads the r59 run JSON, the bench60 tests and joint-profile reports, and supervisor60. None of these is pinned in this manifest, so they fall outside the requester's `unchanged()` check.
- The candidate62 publisher reads policy61 and bench61, which are also unpinned.
- The current bytes are whole, so this does not affect any conclusion, but these files should be pinned as identity records or the dependency removed.
- The terminal-mode verifier does not assert `run.cliVersion` or `requestedModel`.

### Coverage ledger dispositions

| Row | Disposition |
|---|---|
| Policy, parser, serializer, cursors | **Covered.** The parser prefix is reviewed in the whole current policy; historical tests are evidence only. F5 applies. |

export class Refusal extends Error {constructor(code){super(code);this.code=code;}}
export function requireFact(ok,code='PUBLIC_COPY_INVALID'){if(!ok)throw new Refusal(code);}
export function exactObject(value,keys,code='PUBLIC_COPY_INVALID'){
  requireFact(value!==null&&typeof value==='object'&&!Array.isArray(value),code);
  requireFact([Object.prototype,null].includes(Object.getPrototypeOf(value)),code);
  const ds=Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptors(value);
  requireFact(Reflect.ownKeys(ds).length===keys.length&&keys.every(k=>Object.hasOwn(ds,k)&&Object.hasOwn(ds[k],'value')&&ds[k].enumerable),code);
  return value;
}
const profiles=Object.freeze({RAW:Object.freeze({bytes:resourcePolicy.originalStdoutBytes,depth:resourcePolicy.maxEnvelopeDepth,nodes:resourcePolicy.maxEnvelopeNodes}),COPY:Object.freeze({bytes:resourcePolicy.maxCanonicalCopyBytes,depth:64,nodes:200000}),STDOUT:Object.freeze({bytes:resourcePolicy.publicStdoutBytes,depth:resourcePolicy.maxEnvelopeDepth,nodes:resourcePolicy.maxEnvelopeNodes}),RECORD:Object.freeze({bytes:16777216,depth:resourcePolicy.maxEnvelopeDepth,nodes:resourcePolicy.maxEnvelopeNodes})});

function strict(bytes,profile,args){
  requireFact(args===1&&Buffer.isBuffer(bytes),'UNSUPPORTED_SCHEMA');
  requireFact(bytes.length<=profile.bytes,'RESOURCE_PARSE_LIMIT');
  let text;try{text=new TextDecoder('utf8',{fatal:true,ignoreBOM:true}).decode(bytes);}catch{throw new Refusal('JSON_SYNTAX');}
  requireFact(text.charCodeAt(0)!==0xFEFF,'JSON_SYNTAX');
  let at=0,nodes=0;
  const fail=()=>{throw new Refusal('JSON_SYNTAX');};
  const count=()=>{if(++nodes>profile.nodes)throw new Refusal('RESOURCE_PARSE_LIMIT');};
  const ws=()=>{while(at<text.length&&' \n\r\t'.includes(text[at]))at++;};
  function stringToken(){
    if(text[at]!=='"')fail();const start=at++;
    while(at<text.length){const c=text.charCodeAt(at++);if(c===34)return JSON.parse(text.slice(start,at));
      if(c<32)fail();if(c===92){if(at>=text.length)fail();const e=text[at++];if(e==='u'){for(let i=0;i<4;i++)if(!/[0-9a-fA-F]/.test(text[at++]??''))fail();}else if(!'"\\/bfnrt'.includes(e))fail();}}
    fail();
  }
  function value(depth){
    count();ws();const c=text[at];
    if(c==='{'||c==='['){const d=depth+1;if(d>profile.depth)throw new Refusal('RESOURCE_PARSE_LIMIT');at++;ws();
      if(c==='{'){const names=new Set();if(text[at]==='}'){at++;return;}
        for(;;){ws();count();const key=stringToken();if(names.has(key))throw new Refusal('JSON_DUPLICATE');names.add(key);ws();if(text[at++]!==':')fail();value(d);ws();const sep=text[at++];if(sep==='}')return;if(sep!==',')fail();}}
      else{if(text[at]===']'){at++;return;}for(;;){value(d);ws();const sep=text[at++];if(sep===']')return;if(sep!==',')fail();}}}
    if(c==='"'){stringToken();return;}
    for(const token of ['true','false','null'])if(text.startsWith(token,at)){at+=token.length;return;}
    const start=at;if(text[at]==='-')at++;
    if(text[at]==='0')at++;else{if(!/[1-9]/.test(text[at]??''))fail();while(/[0-9]/.test(text[at]??''))at++;}
    if(text[at]==='.'){at++;if(!/[0-9]/.test(text[at]??''))fail();while(/[0-9]/.test(text[at]??''))at++;}
    if(text[at]==='e'||text[at]==='E'){at++;if(text[at]==='+'||text[at]==='-')at++;if(!/[0-9]/.test(text[at]??''))fail();while(/[0-9]/.test(text[at]??''))at++;}
    if(at===start)fail();
  }
  value(0);ws();if(at!==text.length)fail();
  // Only after COMPLETE lexical/resource/duplicate validation create the graph.
  return JSON.parse(text);
}

function serialize(value,profile,pretty,limitCode){
  const chunks=[];let page=Buffer.allocUnsafe(65536),used=0,total=0,nodes=0;const ancestors=new Set();
  function emit(s){const b=Buffer.from(s);requireFact(total+b.length<=profile.bytes,limitCode);total+=b.length;
    let at=0;while(at<b.length){const n=Math.min(page.length-used,b.length-at);b.copy(page,used,at,at+n);used+=n;at+=n;if(used===page.length){chunks.push(page);page=Buffer.allocUnsafe(65536);used=0;}}}
  function string(s){emit('"');let from=0;
    // Emit raw runs in <=16Ki UTF16 chunks without splitting a surrogate pair.
    const flush=end=>{while(from<end){let stop=Math.min(end,from+16384);if(stop<end&&s.charCodeAt(stop-1)>=0xD800&&s.charCodeAt(stop-1)<=0xDBFF)stop--;emit(s.slice(from,stop));from=stop;}};
    for(let i=0;i<s.length;i++){const c=s.charCodeAt(i);let esc=null;
      if(c===34)esc='\\"';else if(c===92)esc='\\\\';else if(c<32)esc=({8:'\\b',9:'\\t',10:'\\n',12:'\\f',13:'\\r'})[c]??'\\u'+c.toString(16).padStart(4,'0');
      else if(c>=0xD800&&c<=0xDBFF){const n=s.charCodeAt(i+1);if(n>=0xDC00&&n<=0xDFFF){i++;continue;}esc='\\u'+c.toString(16);}
      else if(c>=0xDC00&&c<=0xDFFF)esc='\\u'+c.toString(16);
      if(esc!==null){flush(i);emit(esc);from=i+1;}}
    flush(s.length);emit('"');
  }
  const indent=d=>{if(pretty)emit('\n'+'  '.repeat(d));};
  function visit(v,depth){
    requireFact(++nodes<=profile.nodes,'RESOURCE_PARSE_LIMIT');
    if(v===null){emit('null');return;}if(typeof v==='string'){string(v);return;}
    if(typeof v==='boolean'){emit(v?'true':'false');return;}
    if(typeof v==='number'){emit(Number.isFinite(v)?JSON.stringify(v):'null');return;}
    requireFact(typeof v==='object','UNSUPPORTED_SCHEMA');requireFact(depth<profile.depth,'RESOURCE_PARSE_LIMIT');requireFact(!ancestors.has(v),'UNSUPPORTED_SCHEMA');
    const array=Array.isArray(v);requireFact(array||[Object.prototype,null].includes(Object.getPrototypeOf(v)),'UNSUPPORTED_SCHEMA');
    const ds=Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptors(v),keys=Object.keys(v);
    requireFact(Reflect.ownKeys(ds).every(k=>typeof k==='string'&&Object.hasOwn(ds[k],'value')),'UNSUPPORTED_SCHEMA');
    if(array)requireFact(keys.length===v.length&&keys.every((k,i)=>k===String(i)),'UNSUPPORTED_SCHEMA');
    ancestors.add(v);emit(array?'[':'{');
    keys.forEach((k,i)=>{if(i)emit(',');indent(depth+1);if(!array){requireFact(++nodes<=profile.nodes,'RESOURCE_PARSE_LIMIT');string(k);emit(pretty?': ':':');}visit(ds[k].value,depth+1);});
    if(keys.length)indent(depth);emit(array?']':'}');ancestors.delete(v);
  }
  visit(value,0);if(pretty)emit('\n');if(used)chunks.push(page.subarray(0,used));return Buffer.concat(chunks,total);
}
